## SECTOR_EDITOR_HELP

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASECTOR_EDITOR_HELP&lang=en)

### 中文

§H$SECTOR_EDIT_VIEW$§!\n可以通过§H$SECTOR_EDIT_VIEW$§!自由添加或移除在£sector_capital£§Y星域首府§!一定范围内属于你的任意相连恒星系。\n$SEPARATOR$$SEPARATOR$$SEPARATOR$\n§E自动拓展开启时，星域将自动拓展到一定范围内的、还没有归属于另一个星域的所有恒星系中。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:8`

### English

§H$SECTOR_EDIT_VIEW$§!\nUsing the §H$SECTOR_EDIT_VIEW$§! you can freely add or remove any connected system that you own within range of the £sector_capital£§YSector Capital§!.\n$SEPARATOR$$SEPARATOR$$SEPARATOR$\n§EA sector will automatically expand to contain all systems within range that are not already part of a different sector when automatic expansion is enabled.§!

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:10`

## paragon.102.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.102.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[beholder_planet.GetName]上的金字塔已不复存在，原地留下了一个巨大的空洞。当地的未开智生物满脸困惑地站在空洞的峭壁边上。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:25`

### English

The Monolith on [beholder_planet.GetName] has vanished, leaving a giant crater in its wake. The pre-sapient inhabitants line the precipice, seemingly confused.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:30`

## paragon_beholder_category_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon_beholder_category_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们探测到了来自这颗星球的间歇性能量峰值。这些读数表明了一些强大的、远超我们所观察到的未开智物种所具有能力的某种东西。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:28`

### English

We have detected intermittent energy spikes coming from the planet. These readings indicate something powerful, and far beyond the capabilities of the pre-sapient inhabitants we've observed.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:34`

## paragon.3.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们标记出了[From.GetName]上能量波动的源头，它源自一座位于未开智生物领地中心的金字塔形建筑。我们发现当地人口怪异地聚集在建筑周围。\n\n我们若想要确认该物体的功能，则无可避免地会与这颗行星的居民打交道。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:30`

### English

We have pinpointed the source of the energy fluctuations on [From.GetName] to a monolithic structure in the heart of the pre-sapient territory. The local population have been observed curiously congregating around the structure.\n\nIf we are to ascertain the purpose of this object, interaction with the planet's inhabitants is inevitable.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:37`

## paragon.4.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.4.name&lang=en)

### 中文

低语金字塔

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:33`

### English

The Murmuring Monolith

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:41`

## paragon.4.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.4.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的团队开始着手调查[From.From.GetName]上的建筑。它是一座古老的四棱锥结构。科学家汇报在它的周围区域会听到怪异的低语，因此恰如其分地称之为“低语方尖碑”。\n\n这座金字塔似乎在这个星球上的未开智物种所呈现的基本信仰系统中起着不可替代的作用。一个在这种智慧等级的物种甚至还没有开始使用工具时，却在发展复杂的精神实践，这是非比寻常的。\n\n[beholder_presapients.GetAdj]领地意识很强，而且很容易被激怒。我们的到来打扰到了他们，越来越多的生物聚集到了金字塔附近。我们可能需要一些强制措施才能靠近这座建筑了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:34`

### English

Our team has begun investigating the structure on [From.From.GetName]. It is an ancient four-sided pyramid. Scientists report hearing strange whispers in its vicinity and have aptly named it the 'Murmuring Monolith'.\n\nThe Monolith appears to play an integral part in a rudimentary belief system observed by the planet's pre-sapient species. It is unusual to witness a species at this level of sentience developing complex spiritual practices when they have yet to begin using tools.\n\nThe [beholder_presapients.GetAdj] are territorial and easily provoked. Our arrival has disturbed them, and more have begun gathering around the Monolith. Gaining access to the structure will require force.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:42`

## the_beholder_presapients_protected_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Athe_beholder_presapients_protected_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

该行星上的金字塔中涌出的低语令当地的未开智住民感到舒适。他们如同保护自己的生命一般保护这座建筑。我方的任何干扰都可能带来难以预料的后果。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:41`

### English

The whispers emanating from the Monolith on this planet bring comfort to the pre-sapient inhabitants. They protect the structure as if their survival depends on it. Any interference on our part will lead to unpredictable ramifications.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:50`

## BEFRIENDING_BEHOLDER_PRESAPIENTS

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ABEFRIENDING_BEHOLDER_PRESAPIENTS&lang=en)

### 中文

### English

Building Trust

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:54`

## BEFRIENDING_BEHOLDER_PRESAPIENTS_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ABEFRIENDING_BEHOLDER_PRESAPIENTS_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

让[beholder_planet.GetName]上的[beholder_presapients.GetAdj]逐渐适应我们的存在让我们有可能靠近低语金字塔。如果他们降低对我们的警惕我们就能在他们当中自由活动，甚至会有机会进一步研究他们的物种。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:45`

### English

Acclimatizing the [beholder_presapients.GetAdj] on [beholder_planet.GetName] to our presence will help us gain access to the Murmuring Monolith. If they lower their guard around us we can move freely among them, and may also have opportunities to learn more about their species.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:55`

## paragon.5.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.5.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在与[beholder_planet.GetName]上的[beholder_presapients.GetAdj]共处了一段时间后，他们不再那么害怕我们了。我们对其社会结构建立了基本的理解。\n\n$paragon_whispering_info$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:47`

### English

After spending some time with the [beholder_presapients.GetAdj] on [beholder_planet.GetName], they appear less frightened by us. We are building a basic understanding of their social structures.\n\n$paragon_whispering_info$

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:58`

## paragon_whispering_info

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon_whispering_info&lang=en)

### 中文

新的一群[beholder_presapients.GetAdj]开始涌向金字塔。与最初的保卫领土的行为相比，它们现在似乎很平静，甚至有所期待。有什么事情要发生了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:49`

### English

A fresh influx of [beholder_presapients.GetAdj] have begun crowding the Monolith. In contrast with their initial territorial behavior, they now seem to be calm, and even anticipatory. Something is about to happen.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:61`

## paragon.10.name

### 中文

伪神

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:52`

### English

False Gods

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:65`

## paragon.10.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.10.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们已经驯化[beholder_presapients.GetAdj]将我们视为神明。他们现在温顺可欺并向我们寻求庇佑。我们非凡的科技在它们眼中无异于魔法，操纵他们的信仰体系简直信手拈来。我们现在可以自如地前往低语金字塔了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:53`

### English

We have trained the [beholder_presapients.GetAdj] to look at us as deities. They are now domesticated, and reliant on us for safety and protection. Our technological prowess is like magic to them, and manipulating their belief system was an easy task. We now have full access to the Murmuring Monolith.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:66`

## paragon.11.name

## paragon.11.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.11.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们试图驯化[beholder_presapients.GetAdj]，本以为将他们的信仰从金字塔转向我们是轻而易举的事情。不幸的是我们的尝试适得其反，激起他们激烈的反抗。一个巨大的群体开始包围起低语金字塔。我们仍无法接近。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:56`

### English

We attempted to domesticate the [beholder_presapients.GetAdj], assuming we would make short work of refocusing their worship from the Monolith to us. Unfortunately our attempts have backfired, and we have provoked an aggressive response. A great crowd has appeared to blockade the Murmuring Monolith. We cannot gain access.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:69`

## paragon.15.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.15.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在粗暴的火力展示后，我们让[beholder_presapients.GetAdj]被迫意识到我们的优越性。尽管在智力上存在巨大的鸿沟，他们还是吸取教训，明白挡我们的路不符合它们的最佳利益。许多人吓得落荒而逃，回到它们的巢穴中。现在我们可以不受干扰地研究低语金字塔了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:61`

### English

After a bold display of firepower, we have forced the [beholder_presapients.GetAdj] to recognize our superiority. Despite the gulf between our intellects, they have absorbed the lesson that it is not in their best interest to stand in our way. Many have fled in fear, retreating to their dwellings. The Murmuring Monolith can now be examined without interference.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:75`

### English

## WHISPER_TEMPLE_STUDY_DESC

### 中文

我们有机会研究[beholder_planet.GetName]上的远古低语金字塔了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:66`

### English

## paragon.20.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.20.name&lang=en)

### 中文

探索低语金字塔

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:67`

### English

Exploring the Murmuring Monolith

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:83`

## paragon.20.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.20.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们对低语金字塔的研究看来相当耗费时间，而且所获无几。它的秘密仿佛在和我们玩捉迷藏一样。毫无疑问这座四棱锥里一定有什么先进的科技，但它产出的庞大能量以我们目前的科学理解而言是非常怪异的。\n\n在金字塔的外墙之内一定发生着什么特别的事情。$paragon_whispering_info$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:68`

### English

Our study of the Murmuring Monolith has proven time consuming and less revealing than we anticipated. The structure's secrets elude us. There is undeniably advanced technology within the pyramid, but the sheer amount of energy being produced is inconsistent with our current understanding of science.\n\nBeyond the walls of the Monolith, something unusual is going on. $paragon_whispering_info$

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:84`

### 中文

低语呢喃

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:71`

### English

The Murmuring

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:88`

## paragon.25.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.25.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

当所有的[beholder_presapients.GetAdj]都聚集到了低语金字塔周围时，我们观测到了一个前所未见的现象。一种冷静的感觉一瞬间在兽群中涤荡开。这些生物仿佛被什么看不见的力量定住了。我们的一些科学家也被这一不寻常的现象影响，短时间失去了与现实的关联。当他们回过神来时称，他们与那座低语金字塔发生了直接的对话。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:72`

### English

As all the [beholder_presapients.GetAdj] gathered around the Murmuring Monolith, we observed a phenomenon unlike anything we have previously encountered. A sense of calm washed over the crowd. The creatures were transfixed by an invisible force. Some of our scientists were caught up in this paranormal event, and for a moment, lost touch with reality. When they came to, they claimed to have communed directly with the Murmuring Monolith.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:89`

## paragon.25.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.25.a&lang=en)

详细地汇报此事。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:73`

### English

Debrief and report in full.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:90`

## paragon.26.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.26.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

来自我们科学家的关于最近与低语金字塔发生沟通的汇报相当地耐人寻味。据说，这样的通灵经历能让人感受到解放和转变。\n\n科学家将这一事件称为“低语呢喃”，并坚称是那座金字塔直接与他们沟通的。在短暂的接触期间，参与者可以共享他们内心最深处的想法，并被信任和理解的感受所包裹着。\n\n当这一效应结束时，金字塔的能量输出骤降。也许我们应该找到一种重新与金字塔建立联系的方法。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:75`

### English

The report from our scientists regarding the recent communion with the Murmuring Monolith makes for an interesting read. Supposedly, the ordeal was a liberating and transformative experience.\n\nThe scientists now refer to the event as 'The Murmuring', and maintain that the Monolith made direct contact with them. In the brief moment of contact, the participants were able to share their innermost thoughts with all involved, and were overcome by a sense of trust and understanding.\n\nWhen the effects faded, the Monolith's energy emissions plummeted. Perhaps we could devise a way to reestablish contact with the Monolith.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:93`

## WHISPER_TEMPLE_ENERGY

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AWHISPER_TEMPLE_ENERGY&lang=en)

### 中文

低语金字塔：能量

### English

Murmuring Monolith: Energy

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:95`

## WHISPER_TEMPLE_ENERGY_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AWHISPER_TEMPLE_ENERGY_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

通过将能量集中在低语金字塔的内部部件上，我们或许可以重现低语呢喃现象，并实现进一步的接触。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:77`

### English

By concentrating bursts of energy on the internal components of the Murmuring Monolith, we may be able to recreate the Murmuring phenomenon, and achieve further contact.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:96`

## paragon.30.name

### 中文

凝望者

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:78`

### English

The Beholder

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:98`

## paragon.30.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.30.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们成功地复现了第一次低语呢喃时的条件，开启了与金字塔的第二次交流。这次，我们成功地维持了这一现象，并且与一个远在他处利用方尖碑作为媒介的实体进行了接触。\n\n该实体称自己为§Y凝望者§!，声称自己身处§Y虚境§!之内，是在另一个次元利用金字塔进行交流。它以能量为食，同意为我们提供一些帮助来交换供养。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:79`

### English

We successfully recreated the conditions of the first Murmuring, and initiated a second communion with the Monolith. This time we have been able to sustain the phenomenon and have established contact with an entity who is using the Monolith as a conduit to reach out from another location.\n\nThe entity has identified itself as §YThe Beholder§!. It claims to exist within the §YShroud§!, and uses the Monolith to reach out from a different plane of existence. It feeds on energy, and has agreed to offer us assistance in exchange for a feast.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:99`

## paragon.31.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.31.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§H我们按要求提供了能量，完成了和凝望者的交易。能量刚一交付，金字塔就开始发出高频振动。随后它开始摇晃，周围地面开始坍塌。随着巨石上升，它的整体逐渐显露，我们才发现它的本体至少比我们最离谱的估计还要再大上两倍之多。\n\n这座金字塔是一座巨大的八面体结构，它悬浮到了空中，掩盖其上的尘土徐徐落下。随着它飞向[beholder_planet.GetName]大气层的深处，行星的当地居民痛苦地尖叫了起来。\n\n现在我们知道了，低语金字塔是一座拥有太空航行能力的硕大无朋的舰船。它现在正环绕着[beholder_planet.GetName]。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:92`

### English

§HWe fulfilled our end of the bargain with the Beholder, delivering the energy as requested. Upon receipt of the power, the Monolith began emitting high-frequency vibrations. It then began shaking, and the ground surrounding it crumbled, as the Monolith rose, revealing itself to be at least two times larger than even our most outlandish estimates.\n\nA huge octahedral structure, the Monolith was suspended in the air as dirt and dust fell to the ground. The inhabitants of the planet screeched in panic as it took to the skies, climbing high into the atmosphere of [beholder_planet.GetName].\n\nIt is now clear that the Murmuring Monolith is a colossal vessel, capable of spaceflight. It currently orbits [beholder_planet.GetName].§!

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:114`

## paragon.1.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.1.a&lang=en)

### 中文

你需求能量的目的是什么？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:93`

### English

To what end do you seek energy?

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:115`

## paragon.1.a.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.1.a.response&lang=en)

### 中文

§H在长时间的沉默之后，传输频道中渐渐开始被静电噪声填满，它一直变强，听到的人无不被无数的情绪所征服，包括衰弱的遗憾和强烈的欲望等等。有太多的念头无法逐一过滤，但有一个声音可以穿透这一切噪音:§!\n\n是观察。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:94`

### English

§HAfter a long silence, static begins to fill the transmission, steadily increasing until those listening are overcome with a myriad of emotions, from debilitating regrets to compelling desires. Too much to filter through, but for one thing that pierces through the noise:§!\n\nTo observe.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:116`

## paragon.1.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.1.b&lang=en)

### 中文

你可以为我们的文明做什么？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:95`

### English

What can you do for our civilization?

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:117`

## paragon.1.b.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.1.b.response&lang=en)

### 中文

§H一连串不连贯的低语反复回荡。§!\n\n秘密……展露无疑。通过……道路……解开了。的潜力……虚境……开始。我们表示欢迎……让我们……用你的……引导……向那里。\n\n§H尽管这段断断续续的信息可以有多种解释，但毫无疑问，肯定和灵能还有虚境是有关系的。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:96`

### English

§HA string of incoherent, whispering voices echoes forth.§!\n\nSecrets... laid bare. By the potential of... the path... unlocked. The Shroud ... begins. We offer welcome... Let us... use your... guide... there.\n\n§HAlthough the patchy message is open to interpretation, there is little doubt that psionic energy and the Shroud are involved.§!

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:118`

## paragon.1.c.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.1.c.response&lang=en)

### 中文

§H一个声音以惊人的清晰度渐强起来：§!\n\n刹那，与永恒。无相，与万物……你们的语言不够有效。我们类似于“目的”。目前，任何进一步的定义都超出了你的理解范围。虚境无边无垠，你们要学的还有很多。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:98`

### English

§HWith surprising clarity, a single voice swells:§!\n\nFleeting, and eternal. Nothing, and everything... Your language is ineffective. We are akin to 'purpose.' For now, any further definition lies beyond your grasp. The Shroud is vast and there is much to learn.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:120`

## decision_psi_inoculate_pops_custom_tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adecision_psi_inoculate_pops_custom_tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

低语金字塔将逐渐给予£pop£§H人口§!§Y$trait_psionic$§!特质，而不产出£sr_zro£§Y泽珞§!。\n\n§R在进行该过程时无法部署低语金字塔。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:122`

### English

The Murmuring Monolith will grant £pop£ §HPops§! the §Y$trait_psionic$§! Trait over time instead of producing £sr_zro£ §YZro§!.\n\n§RThe Murmuring Monolith cannot be deployed while this is ongoing.§!

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:151`

## decision_psi_inoculate_pops_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adecision_psi_inoculate_pops_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

凝望者会逐渐为人口接种上§Y$trait_psionic$§!特质。这需要人口未在被净化、不是义体人或蜂巢思维。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:123`

### English

The Beholder inoculates Pops over time to become §Y$trait_psionic$§!. This requires that the pops are not being purged, Cybernetic or Hive-Minded.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:152`

## beholder_backstory

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Abeholder_backstory&lang=en)

### 中文

一位虚境实体，虚空之低语的众声之一，通过媒介在不同的存在位面间显灵。尽管祂看起来很良善，但谨慎对待肯定是合理的。其完全的力量和真正的目的尚不得而知。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:139`

### English

A Shroud entity, part of the chorus of the Whisperers, projecting its presence via a conduit between planes of existence. Although it appears benevolent, caution is justified. The full extent of its power, and its true intentions, are unknown.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:176`

## desc_exakeides

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adesc_exakeides&lang=en)

### 中文

他已经在冷冻仓里休眠了无数个纪元。一经解放，§Y艾克萨凯德斯§!想要找回对失去时光的弥补，他的年轻外表下潜藏着无比敏锐的才智。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:144`

### English

For countless eons he lay frozen in cryostasis. Finally free, §YExakeides§! now seeks to make up for lost time, his youthful appearance belying a keen and penetrating intellect.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:183`

## leader_trait_legendary_far_from_home_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_legendary_far_from_home_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

他从哪里来？谁把他冻进了冷冻仓？他又为什么从里面出来了？关于他过去的未解之谜激发了这位领袖对知识的渴求。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:146`

### English

Where does he come from? Who placed him in cryostasis, and why was he released? The unanswered questions of his past fuel this leader's thirst for knowledge.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:185`

## paragon.3100.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3100.name&lang=en)

### 中文

求救信号

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:153`

### English

Distress Signal

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:193`

## paragon.3100.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3100.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

刚一进入[From.GetName]恒星系，我们的传感器就发现了来自[event_target:exakeides_planet.GetName]行星的一个微弱信号。\n\n它具有短促而重复的模式，看起来好像是在求救。然而，由于信号太过微弱，我们估计定位出其来源会相当困难。也许到时候就来不及伸出援手了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:154`

### English

On entering the [From.GetName] system our sensors detected a faint signal originating on planet [event_target:exakeides_planet.GetName].\n\nA short and repeating pattern, it appears to be a call for aid. However, given the signal's weakness, we expect that locating its source will prove difficult. It may already be too late to help.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:194`

## paragon.3100.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3100.a&lang=en)

### 中文

如果还有机会就试试看。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:155`

### English

If there's a chance, we have to take it.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:195`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3100.b&lang=en)

### English

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:196`

## LEGENDARY_LEADER_FIRST_PROJECT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ALEGENDARY_LEADER_FIRST_PROJECT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

需要花费许多时间去定出[event_target:exakeides_planet.GetName]行星上的求救信号准确的地表位置。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:158`

### English

It will take time to pinpoint the exact planetary position of the distress signal on planet [event_target:exakeides_planet.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:198`

## paragon.3104.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3104.name&lang=en)

### 中文

逃生舱

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:159`

### English

Escape Pod

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:199`

## paragon.3104.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3104.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在[event_target:exakeides_planet.GetName]上找到了求救信号的来源。\n\n地壳深处埋藏着一个古老的逃生舱。它的冷冻胶囊仓里似乎有一个外星人幼体。\n\n尽管这个逃生舱已经非常老了，但低温保存设备仍然在运行中，幼体的生命迹象也很稳定。在胶囊的外部粗略地刻着一系列符号。我们将其翻译成：“我们的希望。我们的未来。我们的子孙。”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:160`

### English

We have found the source of the distress signal on [event_target:exakeides_planet.GetName].\n\nBuried deep within the planet's crust was an ancient escape pod. Its cryostasis capsule contained what appears to be an alien child.\n\nDespite the pod's extreme age, the cryopreservation equipment was still functioning, and the child's life signs are stable. On the capsule's exterior a series of symbols were roughly carved. We translated the symbols: 'Our hope. Our future. Our Scion.'

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:200`

## paragon.3104.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3104.a&lang=en)

### 中文

复苏那个孩子。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:161`

### English

Resuscitate the child.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:201`

## LEGENDARY_LEADER_RESUSCITATION

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ALEGENDARY_LEADER_RESUSCITATION&lang=en)

### 中文

冷冻复苏

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:162`

### English

Cryostasis Resuscitation

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:202`

## LEGENDARY_LEADER_RESUSCITATION_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ALEGENDARY_LEADER_RESUSCITATION_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

在[event_target:exakeides_planet.GetName]上发现的孩子在经历了比任何已知历史更久的冷冻期之后成功地活了下来。复苏工作需要小心谨慎的准备。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:163`

### English

The child discovered on [event_target:exakeides_planet.GetName] has survived a longer period of cryostasis than any in recorded history. Resuscitation will require careful and meticulous preparation.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:203`

## paragon.3105.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3105.name&lang=en)

### 中文

复苏成功

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:164`

### English

Successful Resuscitation

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:204`

## paragon.3105.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3105.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在[event_target:exakeides_planet.GetName]上发现的孩子成功复苏了。所有的生命迹象都符合该类生命形式的合理范畴。目前他还没有恢复意识，也许还要一段时间才能苏醒。\n\n在逃生舱内还发现了一些物品。第一件是一块略微损坏的数据水晶，记录了孩子的姓名、年龄和物种。他叫做§Y艾克萨凯德斯§!”，完整的日志表明他还是§Y瓦格罗的子嗣§!。第二件是一个由未知重金属构成的古怪文物。从该文物的设计上看，好像应该属于一个更大装置的一部分。进一步研究已正在计划中。\n\n现在更紧迫的任务是要如何对待这个孩子。不管艾克萨凯德斯到底是，或者曾是什么人，目前来讲他需要照料。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:165`

### English

The child discovered on [event_target:exakeides_planet.GetName] has been successfully resuscitated. All vital signs are within a reasonable range for a lifeform of its type. Still unconscious, it may be some time before the child wakes.\n\nA number of items were also discovered inside the pod. The first was a degraded data crystal which revealed the child's gender, name, and species. He is called §YExakeides§!, and referred to throughout the log as the §Y'Scion of Vagros'§!. The second was a strange relic composed of an unknown, heavy material. Given its design, it is likely that this relic formed a part of a larger whole. Further study is advised.\n\nOf more pressing concern is what to do with the child. Whoever this Exakeides is or was, for the time being he will require care.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:205`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3105.a&lang=en)

### English

## LEGENDARY_LEADER_STUDY_RELIC_PROJECT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ALEGENDARY_LEADER_STUDY_RELIC_PROJECT&lang=en)

### 中文

### English

Relic Fragment

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:210`

## LEGENDARY_LEADER_STUDY_RELIC_PROJECT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ALEGENDARY_LEADER_STUDY_RELIC_PROJECT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

在艾克萨凯德斯的冷冻舱里发现的破碎部件，该遗物远超我们目前的科学理解。需要很长的时间和资源来发现其中的秘密。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:170`

### English

A broken fragment discovered in Exakeides's cryostasis capsule, this relic is well beyond our current scientific understanding. It will take a significant investment of time and resources to uncover its secrets.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:211`

## paragon.3110.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3110.name&lang=en)

### 中文

遗物碎片

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:171`

### English

Relic Fragment

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:212`

## paragon.3110.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3110.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

目前进行中的对艾克萨凯德斯身边的破损遗物的研究表明，它曾与障碍物发生过碰撞。由于丢失的部件过多，我们只能对其真实功能进行猜测。然而，根据近距离观测时记录的异常读数，我们有理由得出结论，该物体对时空有影响。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:172`

### English

Our ongoing research into the broken relic discovered with Exakeides has hit a roadblock. Given how many pieces are missing, we can only speculate on its true purpose. However, based on the anomalous readings we've recorded while in close proximity, there is some reason to conclude that the object has an impact on spacetime.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:213`

## paragon.3114.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3114.name&lang=en)

### 中文

艾克萨凯德斯的成长

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:173`

### English

Exakeides Growing Up

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:214`

## paragon.3114.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3114.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y艾克萨凯德斯§!一直是一个非常早熟的孩子，随着十岁生日到来，他已经开始了更高级的学习。到目前为止，我们的重点一直是鼓励他无限的好奇心，并为他提供一切必要的工具来追求他的兴趣。在未来的几年里，他将由我们所能提供的最优秀的人才来指导。\n\n他有潜力成为科学领域的领军人物，这足以证明我们的付出是会有回报的。问题是，要将他的教育将建立在什么基础上呢？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:174`

### English

Always a remarkably precocious child, §YExakeides's§! tenth year marks the beginning of more advanced studies. Until now, our focus has been on encouraging his boundless curiosity and providing him with all the tools necessary to pursue his interests. In the coming years, he will be tutored by the best minds we have to offer.\n\nHis potential to become a leader in the sciences more than justifies the investment. The question is, upon what foundation will his education proceed?

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:215`

## paragon.3114.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3114.a&lang=en)

### English

## paragon.3114.f

教导他学会服从。

## paragon.3115.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3115.name&lang=en)

### 中文

凯德斯，瓦格罗之子嗣

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:183`

### English

Keides, Scion of Vagros

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:225`

## paragon.3115.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3115.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在二十岁时，§Y艾克萨凯德斯§!已经准备好承担其一位成年人的责任了。\n\n从被复苏那一刻起，他就像一块海绵一样狂热而执着地吸收着我们的教导。尽管他已经具有海量的技艺和无边的智慧，但仍旧谦虚如初。\n\n科学一直是他最热衷的领域，促动着它去发现自己的过往，以及那台与他一同被发现的破碎遗物。在他的同僚中，他的名字被简化为了“§Y凯德斯§!”。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:184`

### English

On turning twenty years old, §YExakeides§! is ready to assume the responsibilities of adulthood.\n\nIn the time since his resuscitation, he has absorbed our teachings with fervor and determination. Despite his prodigious skill and a boundless intellect, he remains humble.\n\nScience has long been his passion, fueling his drive to uncover the truth of his past as well as the purpose of the broken relic he was found with. To his peers, he is known simply as '§YKeides§!'.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:226`

## paragon.3115.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3115.a&lang=en)

### 中文

是时候为他的下一步做规划了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:185`

### English

It is time to plan his next steps.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:227`

## paragon.3115.a.custom_tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3115.a.custom_tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

事件“§Y$paragon.3116.name$§!”将会在§Y10年§!之内发生

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:186`

### English

The event '§Y$paragon.3116.name$§!' happens in §Y10 years.§!

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:228`

## paragon.3116.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3116.name&lang=en)

### 中文

$paragon.3115.name$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:187`

### English

$paragon.3115.name$

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:229`

## paragon.3116.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3116.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

你好。\n\n§H凯德斯昂首站着，观察着形势，也在思考着自己对形势该作何反应。他的目光落在自己的双手上：那双手丝毫没有紧张的迹象，如磐石般沉稳。很明显他为这一天做了充分准备。§!\n\n我准备好了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:188`

### English

Hello.\n\n§HStanding tall, Keides observes the situation and his own response to it. His gaze falls to his hands: they are remarkably steady. He has prepared for this day.§!\n\nI am ready.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:230`

## paragon.3116.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3116.a&lang=en)

### 中文

你好，凯德斯。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:189`

### English

Greetings, Keides.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:231`

## paragon.3116.a.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3116.a.response&lang=en)

### 中文

§H年轻人点了点头，并深吸了一口气。§!\n\n我一直梦想着这么一天。从我记事的那一天起，我就一直期待着这一天。\n\n我已经准备好，献上自己的一份力了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:190`

### English

§HThe young man nods and takes a breath.§!\n\nI have dreamed of this day. I have anticipated it for as long as I can remember.\n\nI stand ready and willing to serve.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:232`

## paragon.3116.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3116.b&lang=en)

### 中文

你会有够长的时间做出贡献的。但首先，你在追寻什么呢？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:191`

### English

There will be enough time for that. But first, what do you seek?

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:233`

## paragon.3116.b.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3116.b.response&lang=en)

### 中文

其他人又在追寻着什么呢？不过是了解自己，了解自己在宇宙中的位置罢了。\n\n我觉得关键在于那个遗物。有了你们的帮助，我就能全身心地投入对其余部分的修复。但请不要误会我：这并不是一个出于自私或者虚荣的请求。我确信，这个物件的力量一定能造福我们的人民。\n\n§H他顿了顿。§!\n\n你们救了我的命，我对你们有所亏欠。但我需要寻找答案，因此需要你们的帮助。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:192`

### English

What does anyone seek? To know themselves, to understand their place in the universe.\n\nI believe the key lies with the relic. With your aid, I will commit myself to recovering the remaining pieces. But do not mistake me - this is no vain quest for selfhood. I am convinced that the power of this object will prove a great boon to our people.\n\n§HHe pauses.§!\n\nI am indebted to you for saving my life. I need to find answers, and I need your help.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:234`

## paragon.3116.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3116.c&lang=en)

### 中文

那么你找到了答案以后呢？那之后又如何？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:193`

### English

And when you find what you seek? What then?

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:235`

## paragon.3116.c.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3116.c.response&lang=en)

### 中文

§H凯德斯自豪地抬起了头。§!\n\n这是一个非常有分量的问题，我也有很多答案。简而言之中，你们给了我一个家。无论这趟旅程会给我带来什么，不论我会学到什么，我都会与你们分享。这方面我说到做到。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:194`

### English

§HKeides looks up proudly.§!\n\nA weighted question, with many answers. In short, you have given me a home. Wherever this journey takes me, whatever I learn, I will share it with you. On that you have my word.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:236`

## paragon.3117.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3117.name&lang=en)

### 中文

遗物的影响

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:195`

### English

The Relic's Influence

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:238`

## paragon.3117.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3117.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

对遗物进行初步调查时，§Y凯德斯§!汇报了一段不同寻常的经历。\n\n在进行物理接触后，他的脑海中出现了“幻视”；出现了人和地方的画面，是被埋在遥远过往的回忆。尽管他尚未完全理解其中的含义，但其中的一些画面描绘的是现实的地点。\n\n凯德斯请求发起一个特殊项目对其进行探索。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:196`

### English

Upon initial investigation of the relic, §YKeides§! has reported an unusual experience.\n\nAfter physical contact, 'visions' have appeared in his mind; images of people and places, memories of a long forgotten past. Although he has yet to discern their full meaning, some of these visions depict real locations.\n\nKeides is calling for a Special Project to explore them.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:239`

## paragon.3117.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3117.a&lang=en)

### 中文

有趣。我们会找到答案的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:197`

### English

Curious. We must find answers.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:240`

## paragon_legendary_leader_chain_desc

### 中文

§Y凯德斯§!发来的一份正式请求，希望验证他的幻视是否是真实有意义的。\n\n如果同意就有可能解锁他的过去和与他一同找到的神秘遗物的秘密。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:199`

### English

A formal request from §YKeides§! to prove or disprove the validity of his visions.\n\nDoing so may well unlock the key to his past and the mysterious relic he was found with.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:243`

## legendary_leader_system_1_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Alegendary_leader_system_1_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

熔化的岩石透出炽热的红光，这颗星球的表面被板块运动撕得粉碎。\n\n据信位于[event_target:1st_system_legendary.GetName]星系。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:202`

### English

Aglow with molten rock, the surface of this world has been shattered by tectonic activity.\n\nReportedly in the [event_target:1st_system_legendary.GetName] system.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:246`

## legendary_leader_system_2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Alegendary_leader_system_2&lang=en)

## legendary_leader_system_2_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Alegendary_leader_system_2_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一颗丛林密布的行星，潮湿的阴影和波荡的林冠栖息着捕食者……和他们的猎物。\n\n据信位于[event_target:2nd_system_legendary.GetName]星系。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:204`

### English

A planet of lush jungle, the dripping shadows and waving canopy are alive with predators... and their prey.\n\nReportedly in the [event_target:2nd_system_legendary.GetName] system.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:248`

## legendary_leader_system_3

## legendary_leader_system_3_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Alegendary_leader_system_3_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

满地的弹坑，任由狂风侵蚀。这颗行星的荒凉地表上四处都是坠毁的飞行器。\n\n据信位于[event_target:3rd_system_legendary.GetName]星系。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:206`

### English

Scarred with craters and ravaged by winds, this planet's barren surface is littered with desiccated spacecraft.\n\nReportedly in the [event_target:3rd_system_legendary.GetName] system.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:250`

## paragon.3120.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3120.name&lang=en)

幻视成真

### English

Substantiated Vision

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:252`

## paragon.3120.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3120.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

进入[From.GetName]星系之后不久，我们很快就定位了一颗符合§Y凯德斯§!幻视的行星。在这颗行星的表面流淌着熔矿的海洋。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:208`

### English

Shortly after entry into the [From.GetName] system, a planet matching §YKeides§!'s vision has been located. Oceans of molten minerals stream across the planet's surface.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:253`

## paragon.3120.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3120.a&lang=en)

### 中文

准备探索行星。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:209`

### English

Prepare for planetary exploration.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:254`

## LEGENDARY_LEADER_VOLCANO_PLANET_PROJECT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ALEGENDARY_LEADER_VOLCANO_PLANET_PROJECT&lang=en)

### 中文

火山探索

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:210`

### English

Volcanic Exploration

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:255`

## LEGENDARY_LEADER_VOLCANO_PLANET_PROJECT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ALEGENDARY_LEADER_VOLCANO_PLANET_PROJECT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

对$NAME_Vapob$恒星系中的这颗行星的初步调查证实了§Y凯德斯§!的幻视中闪现的场景。\n\n尽管地质不稳定，但此地仍有一些相互隔绝的坚实地面。探险还是有可能进行的。\n\n§R该特殊项目必须由§Y凯德斯§!本人施行。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:211`

### English

Initial investigation of the planet in the $NAME_Vapob$ system corroborates with the scenes glimpsed in §YKeides§!'s vision.\n\nThough geologically unstable, there are isolated areas of firm ground. An expedition is possible.\n\n§RThis Special Project must be researched by §YKeides§! himself.§!

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:256`

## paragon.3121.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3121.name&lang=en)

### 中文

火山探索

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:212`

### English

Volcanic Exploration

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:257`

## paragon.3121.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3121.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在惊涛拍岸的熔岩海洋中，§Y凯德斯§!发现了一处矿物处理设施的遗迹。他报告有新的幻视：一条黑暗的走廊，通向一个密封的房间。根据这些影像，凯德斯找到了一个古老的、腐朽的盒子。盒子里面有一块残片，完全没有受到时间的摧残。\n\n凯德斯汇报称，在接触到它的一瞬间，新的令人困惑的幻视立刻向他扑面而来。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:213`

### English

Between the bays and swells of the planet's molten oceans, §YKeides§! discovered the remains of a mineral processing facility. He reported new visions of a dark corridor that led to a sealed room. Following these images led Keides to an ancient and moldering box. Inside the box, he found a fragment of the relic, untouched by the ravages of time.\n\nKeides reports that upon touching it he was struck by a new and troubling vision.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:258`

## paragon.3121.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3121.a&lang=en)

### 中文

他看到了什么？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:214`

### English

What did he see?

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:259`

## paragon.3122.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3122.name&lang=en)

### 中文

往昔魅影

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:215`

### English

Ghosts of the Past

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:260`

## paragon.3122.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3122.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在§Y凯德斯§!的身边浮现出了一座巨大熔炉的轧制流程。矿物、工厂、载具覆盖着大地，航母编队在空中遮天蔽日。在噪音之外，还有数量惊人的传送门，忽明忽灭，可以折叠起来。突然，天地间传来一声巨大的轰鸣，连大地都开始震颤。远处传来一阵绞痛的尖叫声。\n\n从视野中恢复过来后，凯德斯将新的碎片与现有的碎片放在一起。一声尖锐的啸叫充满了空气。几乎就在同时，这两块碎片就自动融合在一起了。于是这件遗珍的影响变得更加强烈了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:216`

### English

The milling activity of a great furnace rose around §YKeides§!. Mines, factories, and vehicles blanketed the ground. Huge carrier vessels dotted the skies. Beyond the noise, there was a staggering number of portals, blinking in and out of existence, folding in on themselves. Suddenly there was a great rumbling, and the ground began to shake. A strangled scream came from the distance.\n\nAfter recovering from his vision, Keides set the new fragment alongside the existing piece. A sharp whine filled the air. Almost instantly, the two parts fused together of their own accord. The relic's effects appear strengthened.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:261`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3125.desc&lang=en)

追踪着§Y凯德斯§!的幻视，我们来到了一颗位于[From.GetName]恒星系的行星。茂密的丛林覆盖了地面的90%。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:219`

Following §YKeides§!'s vision has led us to a planet in the [From.GetName] system. Dense jungle covers more than 90% of its land area.

### 中文

如果这颗位于$NAME_Taprib$恒星系的行星和§Y凯德斯§!幻视中所见的一般，那么我们在这里应该小心再小心。\n\n§R该特殊项目必须由凯德斯本人研究。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:222`

### English

If the planet in the $NAME_Taprib$ system is anything like the one §YKeides§! saw in his vision, we should exercise extreme caution.\n\n§RThis Special Project must be researched by Keides himself.§!

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:268`

## paragon.3126.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3126.name&lang=en)

### 中文

林冠之上

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:223`

### English

Above the Canopy

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:269`

## paragon.3126.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3126.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

野生丛林存在重重危险，要想安全地导航是十分困难的。§Y凯德斯§!不止一次从鬼门关逃了出来。\n\n尽管如此，在一种神秘力量的吸引下，他仍继续前进，终于发现了一座巨大建筑的杂草丛生的遗迹。一个摇摇欲坠的楼梯井通向一座远高于林冠的基座。在那里的黑色金属底座上，有另一块遗物碎片。\n\n几乎就在一瞬间，§Y凯德斯§!被拉入了幻视场景中。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:224`

### English

The perils of the wild jungle have proven difficult to safely navigate. More than once, §YKeides§! has narrowly escaped the jaws of death.\n\nEven so, drawn by a mysterious force, he pressed on, only to discover the overgrown remnants of a massive structure. A crumbling stairwell led to a pedestal far above the canopy. There, resting on a plinth of black metal, was another fragment of the relic.\n\nAlmost instantly, §YKeides§! was plunged into a vision.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:270`

## paragon.3126.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3126.a&lang=en)

### 中文

他看到了什么？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:225`

### English

What did he see?

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:271`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3127.name&lang=en)

### 中文

往昔魅影

### English

Ghosts of the Past

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:272`

## paragon.3127.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3127.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

树木都散开了，取而代之的是一片苍翠的景色。这是一颗美丽而温顺的星球，有大型森林一般大小的动物围栏，包含了来自星系各处的物种。\n\n突然，幻视把§Y凯德斯§!扯入进了一座大型建筑内的一个房间。其中一面墙描绘着数个恒星系的星图。其中四个星系与其他的明显不同，没有超空间航道连接。\n\n从幻视中回到船上，§Y凯德斯§!把最新的一块遗物放在现有的部分旁边。它们发出嗡嗡声，肉眼可见地相互吸引，然后融合在一起。遗物的效果似乎加强了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:227`

### English

The vegetation peeled back, giving way to a green vista. A beautiful, tamed world, with animal enclosures the size of large forests, containing species from all over the galaxy.\n\nSuddenly, the vision jolted §YKeides§! into a room inside a larger building. One wall depicted a map of several star systems. Four stood out clearly from the rest, unconnected by hyperlanes.\n\nBack aboard the ship,§YKeides§! set the newest piece of the relic alongside the existing one. Humming, and visibly drawn towards one another, they fused. The relic appears strengthened.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:273`

### English

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ALEGENDARY_LEADER_SHIP_PLANET_PROJECT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在$NAME_Ivusheh$恒星系的一颗行星上观测到一座舰船的坟场，与§Y凯德斯§!的幻视非常相似。\n\n§R该特殊项目必须由§Y凯德斯§!本人研究。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:233`

### English

A graveyard of ships, eerily similar to the one in §YKeides§!'s vision, has been observed on a planet in the $NAME_Ivusheh$ system.\n\n§RThis Special Project must be researched by §YKeides§! himself.§!

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:280`

## paragon.3131.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3131.name&lang=en)

### 中文

舰船坟场探索

### English

Ship Graveyard Exploration

## paragon.3131.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3131.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

行星的碎裂表面四散着破旧的舰船。在最大的一处残骸堆中心，§Y凯德斯§!发现了一座巨大恒星基地的主框架。\n\n把他带到这颗行星一样的不可抗拒的力量又出现了，牵引着§Y凯德斯§!走向基地内的一个房间。在被尘封的货仓中是遗物的另一个碎片。\n\n在触碰到新发现的碎片的一瞬间，§Y凯德斯§!被拉入了一个幻视当中。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:235`

### English

The planet's broken surface is littered with battered spacecraft. At the center of the largest debris field, §YKeides§! discovered the framework of a colossal starbase.\n\nWith irresistible power, the pull of whatever led §YKeides§! to the planet drew him to a single room inside the base. Among the dust-covered cargo waited another piece of the relic.\n\nUpon touching the newfound fragment, §YKeides§! was plunged into another vision.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:282`

## paragon.3131.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3131.a&lang=en)

### 中文

他这次看到了什么？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:236`

### English

What did he see this time?

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:283`

## paragon.3132.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3132.name&lang=en)

### 中文

往昔魅影

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:237`

### English

Ghosts of the Past

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:284`

## paragon.3132.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3132.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一座围绕着这颗行星的恒星基地。其船坞中源源不断地涌出战列舰的编队。\n\n在旗舰的甲板上，站立着一位年轻的舰队司令，一手握着一件与遗物相仿的物体。随着他手腕的一挥，飞船外的空间模糊、变暗，然后逐渐折叠显现出一个黑洞。当这位舰队司令命令舰队进入黑暗时，幻象突然结束了。\n\n§Y凯德斯§!带着碎片回到船上，把它放在现有的碎片旁边。它们都开始发出刺耳的嗡嗡声振动起来。然后，在某种未知力量的作用下，两块融合成一体。事后，遗物的效果似乎加强了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:238`

### English

A vast starbase orbited the planet. From its docks flowed an unending procession of battleships.\n\nOn the deck of the flagship a young admiral stood, grasping in one hand an object resembling the relic. With a flick of his wrist, the space outside his ship blurred, darkened, then folded into itself as a black hole appeared. As the admiral ordered his fleet into the darkness, the vision abruptly ended.\n\nReturning aboard with the fragment, §YKeides§! placed it next to the existing piece. Both began to vibrate with a piercing hum. Then, by some unknown force, the two pieces fused into one. After the fact, the relic's effects appear strengthened.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:285`

## paragon.3132.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3132.a&lang=en)

### English

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3135.name&lang=en)

### 中文

复原遗物

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:240`

### English

Restored Relic

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:288`

## paragon.3135.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3135.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在取回最后一块碎片后，遗物恢复如初，仿佛从未发生破碎一样。\n\n随着遗物复原，对其目的进行研究迫在眉睫，且随时可以准备就绪。一项由§Y凯德斯§!抓总的特殊项目已经准备就绪。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:241`

### English

After the retrieval of the final fragment, the relic appears as if it was never broken.\n\nUpon the relic's restoration, research on its purpose is promising and ready to continue. A Special Project led by §YKeides§! has been prepared.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:289`

## paragon.3135.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3135.a&lang=en)

### 中文

这可是件要紧事。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:242`

### English

A priority endeavor.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:290`

## LEGENDARY_LEADER_OPEN_WORMHOLE_PROJECT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ALEGENDARY_LEADER_OPEN_WORMHOLE_PROJECT&lang=en)

### 中文

复原遗物

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:243`

### English

Restored Relic

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:291`

## LEGENDARY_LEADER_OPEN_WORMHOLE_PROJECT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ALEGENDARY_LEADER_OPEN_WORMHOLE_PROJECT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

重重谜题围绕着§Y凯德斯§!所复原的遗物，而这是一次解答这些谜题的尝试。这是他幻视的来源吗？如果是这样，那么这件遗物还具有什么能力呢？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:244`

### English

An endeavor to answer the questions surrounding §YKeides§!'s restored relic. Was it indeed the cause of his visions? And if so, what further powers does it possess?

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:292`

## paragon.3140.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3140.name&lang=en)

### 中文

遗物研究

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:245`

### English

Relic Research

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:293`

## paragon.3140.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3140.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

对最近复原的遗物进行的研究带来了一些引人注目的发现。\n\n它确实与§Y凯德斯§!有所联系：当与其瓦格罗基因接触时，遗物中的粒子会开始高频共振。尽管这背后的机制尚不清楚，但这种效应是时空操纵所体现的特征。\n\n在数次尝试测量并控制该过程后，§Y凯德斯§!经历了又一次幻视。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:246`

### English

Research into the recently restored relic has led to some intriguing discoveries.\n\nIts connection to §YKeides§! has been affirmed. When in contact with his Vagrosian DNA, the relic's particles begin to resonate at high frequencies. Although the mechanics behind this remain unknown, the effects are symptomatic of space-time manipulation.\n\nAfter several attempts to measure and control this process, §YKeides§! experienced another vision.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:294`

## paragon.3140.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3140.a&lang=en)

### 中文

报告！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:247`

### English

Report!

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:295`

## paragon.3141.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3141.name&lang=en)

### 中文

楔

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:248`

### English

The Key

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:296`

## paragon.3141.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3141.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y凯德斯§!描述了一种仿佛从里面看到遗物的感觉。恒星大小的粒子在他周围旋转。在两者间的虚空中，他经感受到一股力量涌了上来。仅凭双手，他就能指挥球体的运动。\n\n§Y凯德斯§!从幻象中回来，把手放在了遗物上。现在他已经了解了它的用途，以及它惊人的能力：如果控制得当，它能使空间中的两点坍缩，创造一对稳定的虫洞。瓦格罗人称这种装置为§Y楔§!。\n\n在他的发现的激励下，§Y凯德斯§!主动在我们的母星系中打开了一个虫洞。连他自己都不知道这条路通向哪里，但他仍然坚定，相信这很可能是他族人的家园。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:249`

### English

§YKeides§! describes seeing the relic as if from within. Particles the size of suns swirled around him. In the void between, he experienced a surge of power. With his hands alone, he commanded the movements of the spheres.\n\nReturning from this vision, §YKeides§! placed his hands on the relic. Now he understood its use, and the astonishing scope of its power. When properly controlled, it has the ability to collapse two points in space, effectively creating a stable wormhole. The Vagrosians called this device a §YKey§!.\n\nSpurred by his discovery, §YKeides§! acted on his own initiative to open a wormhole in our home system. Not even he knows where it leads, but he remains resolute, believing it is likely his people's home.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:297`

## paragon.3141.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3141.a&lang=en)

### 中文

让我们尽快找到答案吧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:250`

### English

Let us find out, sooner rather than later.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:298`

## paragon.3144.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3144.name&lang=en)

### 中文

敌对舰船！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:251`

### English

Hostile Fleet!

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:300`

## paragon.3144.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3144.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

遗物所生成的虫洞的另一端是$NAME_Sursect$星系。进入后不久，我们的传感器发现了一队有敌意的无人机。它们似乎在守护着这个星系唯一的一颗行星。初步的扫描结果显示，那些无人机上没有生命形式的信号，行星上同样也没有。\n\n§Y凯德斯§!非常确信那颗行星就是他们民族的起源地。毫无疑问答案就在那里。\n\n随后，无人机群向我们发出了通讯要求。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:252`

### English

At the other end of the relic-spawned wormhole lies the $NAME_Sursect$ system. Shortly after entry, our sensors picked up a fleet of hostile drones. They appear to be keeping vigil over the system's single planet. Initial scans suggest there are no life forms present among them, nor on the planet itself.\n\n§YKeides§! has stated with certainty that the planet is indeed the origin of his people. It is sure to hold answers.\n\nA demand from the drones to open comms follows.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:301`

打开通讯。

### English

Open comms.

## paragon.3145.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3145.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§H随着频道开启，尖锐的静电噪音与机器人模拟语音混合在一起。在一个周期内，每个声音都与下一个声音重叠，信息的音量在不规则的波中振荡。§!\n\n动机：回收楔，违反章程。\n瓦格罗之楔，状态：未收集。\n瓦格罗帝国章程第412条，状态：违反。\n\n目标行为：归还楔。\n应对方式：摧毁目标。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:255`

### English

§HAs the channel opens, screeching static blends with a wash of robotic vocalization. In cycles each voice overlaps the next, the message's volume oscillating in irregular waves.§!\n\nIMPETUS: key confiscation, charter breach.\nVAGROSIAN KEY, STATUS: uncollected.\nVAGROS-IMPERIAL CHARTER NO. 412, STATUS: in breach.\n\nTARGET ACTION: return key.\nCOUNTERACTION: destroy target.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:304`

遗物是我们的。另寻他法。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:256`

### English

The relic is ours. Find another way.

### English

Give them the Key. Our safety is the priority.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:306`

## paragon.3146.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3146.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§H合成语音的吱嘎声逐渐放慢，暂时停顿，然后继续说出最后几句话。§!\n\n瓦格罗之楔，状态：紧急收集。\n瓦格罗帝国章程第412条，状态：暂时忽略。\n\n行为：待机。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:259`

### English

§HThe crackle of artificial voices is slowed, halting momentarily, then continues.§!\n\nVAGROSIAN KEY, STATUS: collection imminent.\nVAGROS-IMPERIAL CHARTER NO. 412, STATUS: tentatively omitted.\n\nACTION: hold.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:308`

失去遗珍

## paragon.3147.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3147.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

将楔交给那支舰队后，那些无人机立刻顺着一个它们自己的虫洞离开了。\n\n在那之后，虫洞在它们身后关上了。我们现在可以自由地探索这个恒星系和里面的行星了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:262`

### English

Upon handing over the Key to the fleet, the drones promptly departed through a wormhole of their own.\n\nAfterwards, the wormhole closed behind them. We are now free to explore the system and its planet.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:312`

我们做出了正确的选择。

### English

We made the right choice.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:313`

## LEGENDARY_LEADER_SABOTAGE_FLEET_PROJECT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ALEGENDARY_LEADER_SABOTAGE_FLEET_PROJECT&lang=en)

电磁干扰

### English

Electromagnetics

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:315`

## LEGENDARY_LEADER_SABOTAGE_FLEET_PROJECT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ALEGENDARY_LEADER_SABOTAGE_FLEET_PROJECT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y凯德斯§!发起的一项特殊项目，旨在缓解$NAME_Sursect$恒星系中无人机的哨戒。\n\n他打算用电磁场摧毁其电源。如果成功的话我们就能去到这个星系的的行星上了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:265`

### English

A Special Project prompted by §YKeides§!, to relieve the $NAME_Sursect$ system's drones of their vigil.\n\nHe intends to deploy an electromagnetic field to knock out their power sources. A successful outcome should afford us access to the system's planet.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:316`

## paragon.3148.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3148.name&lang=en)

### 中文

电磁干扰成功

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:266`

### English

Electromagnetic Success

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:317`

## paragon.3148.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3148.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们发射了一股电磁脉冲，烧毁了这些无人机的电路。\n\n我们现在可以自由探索$NAME_Sursect$恒星系和里面的行星了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:267`

### English

We delivered an electromagnetic pulse which fried the circuits of the drones.\n\nWe are now free to explore the $NAME_Sursect$ system and its planet.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:318`

## paragon.3148.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3148.a&lang=en)

任务成功。

### English

A successful mission.

## paragon.3149.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3149.name&lang=en)

### 中文

摧毁无人机

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:269`

### English

Drones Destroyed

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:321`

## paragon.3149.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3149.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管无人机群被击败了，但是它们的制造者也留了一手，让摧毁它们的人也捞不到什么好处。无人机在回应我们的攻击时，启动了自毁程序。残余的东西难以被打捞。\n\n不过，我们现在可以自由地探索$NAME_Sursect$恒星系和里面的行星了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:270`

### English

Despite the drones' defeat, whoever created them made sure that no one could benefit from their destruction. The drones initiated self-destruct protocols in response to our attack. What little remains is hardly salvageable.\n\nStill, we are now free to explore the $NAME_Sursect$ system and its planet.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:322`

## paragon.3149.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3149.a&lang=en)

### 中文

这仍然算得上是一场胜利。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:271`

### English

This still counts as a victory.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:323`

## paragon_legendary_leader_homeworld

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon_legendary_leader_homeworld&lang=en)

### 中文

瓦格罗人废墟

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:272`

### English

Vagrosian Ruins

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:325`

## paragon_legendary_leader_homeworld_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon_legendary_leader_homeworld_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

那些无人机所保护的是一颗被潮汐锁定的行星，一面向着夜空的黑暗与严寒，另一面则沐浴着永恒的光明。在温暖的昼半球，我们发现了一座可能曾是瓦格罗人城市的废墟。\n\n§Y凯德斯§!相信，他过往的细节可能就深埋于此，并且希望在这里的发现能够揭示这颗行星的历史。\n\n§R这个遗址必须由§Y凯德斯§!亲自挖掘。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:273`

### English

The planet the drones were protecting is tidally locked, one side facing the cold darkness of space, and the other bathed in perpetual light. On the warm, light-filled half of this world we uncovered the ruins of what may be a Vagrosian city.\n\n§YKeides§! trusts that details of his past are buried here, and hopes the findings might reveal the planet's history.\n\n§RThis site must be excavated by §YKeides§! himself.§!

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:326`

## paragon.3150.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3150.name&lang=en)

### 中文

行政中心？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:274`

### English

Seat of Power?

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:327`

## paragon.3150.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3150.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这颗行星在瓦格罗社会中一定扮演了至关重要的角色。在挖掘初期，很明显，大部分基础设施都以行政工作为中心，这座城市可能是一个通信枢纽。这里还有许多领导层碰面的地方，而且已经发现了几个极大规模的公共广场。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:275`

### English

The planet must have played a vital role in the Vagrosian society. Early into the excavations, it has become clear that much of the infrastructure was centered around administrative work, the city potentially serving as a hub for communications. There was no shortage of places for those in leadership to gather, and several public squares of truly massive proportions have already been uncovered.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:328`

## paragon.3150.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3150.a&lang=en)

### 中文

很不错。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:276`

### English

Promising.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:329`

## paragon.3151.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3151.name&lang=en)

### 中文

保险库

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:277`

### English

The Vault

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:330`

## paragon.3151.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3151.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

因为很幸运地发现了一座地下保险库，§Y凯德斯§!兴奋得几天没睡着。他确信这个地方就隐藏着自己身世的秘密。\n\n在保险库的深处，他发现了一排又一排的冷冻仓，但里面都是空的。集成在地板上的电路连向了一座更大的结构：在拱顶的中心有一个像王座一样的基座。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:278`

### English

Thanks to the fortunate discovery of an underground vault, §YKeides§! has not slept for days. He is convinced that this place holds the key to his existence.\n\nDeep within the vault, he discovered row upon row of cryostasis pods, all of them empty. Circuits integrated in the floor led to a larger structure: a throne-like pedestal at the very center of the vault.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:331`

## paragon.3151.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3151.a&lang=en)

### 中文

真有意思。这是干什么用的？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:279`

### English

Intriguing. What is its purpose?

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:332`

## paragon.3152.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3152.name&lang=en)

### 中文

强制幻视

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:280`

### English

Forceful Visions

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:333`

## paragon.3152.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3152.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

不顾科学小组的抗议，§Y凯德斯§!毅然决定坐上那台王座般的机器。\n\n他刚一坐下来就失去了知觉。\n\n尽管他的表情变得放松，但他的身体却承受着剧烈的痉挛。科学小组再怎么努力也没能把他从王座上解救出来。几个小时过去了。最后，就在他年轻的身体似乎再也无法承受的时候，凯德斯醒了。他的报告即将发表。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:281`

### English

Against the protestations of the science team, §YKeides§! made the decision to sit upon the throne-like machine.\n\nNo sooner had he sat down, he lost consciousness.\n\nEven as his expression slackened, his body was wracked by convulsions. No amount of effort from the science team was able to free him from the throne. Hours passed. Finally, just when it seemed his young body could bear no more, Keides awoke. His report is forthcoming.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:334`

## paragon.3152.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3152.a&lang=en)

### 中文

真是一如既往地莽撞。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:282`

### English

Bold as ever.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:335`

## paragon.3153.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3153.name&lang=en)

### 中文

幻视报告其一

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:283`

### English

First Vision Report

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:336`

## paragon.3153.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3153.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y凯德斯§!看到了一座广场。广场上有成百上千个人。\n\n大家面朝天空，看着一艘巨大的飞船从大气层外徐徐降落。就在此时一个声音响起：\n\n“听着”它说，“审判之日就要到了。楔将会被夺走。你们应留在此地，直到一切的终结。”\n\n人们的哭喊震耳欲聋。在他的幻视中，凯德斯意识到他的星球已经被抛弃了。他们的主人，一个拥有难以想象的力量的外星物种，发现他的人民缺乏潜力。楔（及其产生的虫洞）是连接他的星球和更广阔的星系的唯一途径。一旦与星系贸易切断往来，他的人民繁荣的基础就无可挽回地丧失了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:284`

### English

§YKeides§! saw a public square. Packed inside it were thousands, if not hundreds of thousands of people.\n\nFaces turned to the sky, they watched as a massive ship descended from the upper atmosphere. At once a single voice boomed out.\n\n'Attend,' it said. 'For the time of judgment is at hand. The Keys will be taken. Here you shall remain, until the end of all things.'\n\nThe cries of the people were deafening. In the midst of his vision, Keides understood that his world had been abandoned. Their masters, an off-world species of unimaginable power, had found his people lacking. The Keys (and the stable wormholes they produced) were all that connected his world to the wider galaxy. Cut off from galactic trade, the foundation of his people's prosperity was suddenly, and irretrievably, lost.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:337`

## paragon.3153.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3153.a&lang=en)

### 中文

真是严苛的惩罚。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:285`

### English

A severe punishment.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:338`

## paragon.3154.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3154.name&lang=en)

### 中文

幻视报告其二

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:286`

### English

Second Vision Report

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:339`

## paragon.3154.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3154.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

作为怜悯，瓦格罗人被宽限一个月内交出所有楔和相关材料。事实上，这与其说是怜悯，不如说是侮辱，因为这点时间根本不够他们自救。作为一颗行政管理行星是没有办法维持其庞大人口的。他们所能期望的最好结果就是定量配给他们所拥有的食物，让自己能晚一点挨饿。他们在外面的主人当然了然于胸。\n\n然而，即使在绝望中，这颗星球的领袖也想出了一个计划。最近对高级冷冻保存和基因组学的试验结果基于了给了他们一个不一定能成功的机会。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:287`

### English

As a mercy, the Vagrosians were given a month to yield all Keys and related materials. In truth, it was more insult than mercy and not nearly sufficient to save themselves. As an administrative world, the planet did not have the means to sustain its vast population. The best they could hope for was to ration what they had, delaying the time of their starvation. Their off-world masters were well aware of this.\n\nHowever, even in their despair, the planet's leaders hatched a plan. Recent forays into advanced cryopreservation and genomics had afforded them an unlikely opportunity.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:340`

## paragon.3154.a

### 中文

他们没有在沉默中死亡。肃然起敬。

### English

So, they would not go quietly. Commendable.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:341`

## paragon.3155.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3155.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

当瓦格罗人的求生计划开始实施时，包含他们未来的“子嗣”§Y凯德斯§!被发射到太空中，同时被发射的还有他们最后一个楔的碎片，它被分解成四块，四散到星系各处以避免被发现。凯德斯出生时就没有父母，而是基因工程的产物。他的DNA中包含着对他的族人的记忆。当他成年时，只要接触到楔就可以通过幻视来获取这些记忆。\n\n除了这个计划，这个行星的领袖还下令建造数千个冷冻舱和巨大的地下空间来容纳它们。在那里，最聪明的瓦格罗人可以一直休眠，等待“子嗣”的归来。他们确实如此，直到低温冷冻也无法继续支撑他们的身体。他们心智化为数据，在王位里等待着子嗣的归来。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:290`

### English

As the Vagrosian survival plan was set into motion, the pod containing their would-be 'Scion' §YKeides§! was launched into space - and with it, a fragment of their last Key, broken into four pieces and sent across the galaxy to avoid detection. Keides was born without parents, the product of genetic engineering. Encoded within his very DNA lay the memories of his people. Upon his maturity, contact with the Key would allow him to access these memories in the form of visions.\n\nAlongside this plan, the planet's leaders ordered the construction of thousands of cryostasis pods and the giant underground space to house them. There, the brightest Vagrosian minds could sleep, awaiting the Scion's return. And so they had, until the cryostasis could no longer support their bodies. Minds reduced to data, they waited for their Scion inside his throne.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:343`

### English

发掘完成

### English
