## paragon.3156.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3156.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

为了不让他们白白牺牲，§Y凯德斯§!再次坐回到子嗣王座上。结果这一次事情很快并且一点也不剧烈地就完成了。他的人民的记忆就像溪水一样平静地涌进他的脑海。\n\n有了他祖先的知识和记忆，挖掘工作很快就结束了。凯德斯的下一步行动是处理所有获得的数据，然后撰写报告。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:293`

### English

So as not to squander their sacrifice, §YKeides§! once more sat upon the Scion's throne. This time it proved a shorter and less dramatic affair. The memories of his people entered him with the tranquility of a flowing stream.\n\nWith his ancestors' knowledge and memories in hand, the excavation was shortly concluded. Keides's next course of action is to process all the obtained data and then report back.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:346`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3156.a&lang=en)

很好。我们期待他的汇报。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:294`

### English

Good. We await his report.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:347`

## paragon.3160.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3160.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§H凯德斯的耷拉着肩，失去了一贯的活力。最近的事件似乎让他倍感疲惫。不过，他依然面带释然的微笑。§!\n\n我得到了答案。其中有好有坏，但我很高兴找到了它们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:296`

### English

§HKeides's shoulders are slumped, his usual vigor diminished. It would seem recent events have taken their toll. Still, he wears an easy smile.§!\n\nI have my answers. They may be bittersweet, but I am glad to know them.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:350`

## paragon.3160.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3160.a&lang=en)

### 中文

你没有让列祖列宗丢脸。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:297`

### English

You do your ancestors proud.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:351`

## paragon.3160.a.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3160.a.response&lang=en)

### 中文

我只能尽力而为不辜负他们的期望。这份责任实在是太重大了，但我很愿意承担。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:298`

### English

I can only try to live up to their expectations. It is a great responsibility, but one I willingly accept.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:352`

## paragon.3160.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3160.b&lang=en)

### 中文

楔怎么样了？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:299`

### English

What of the Key?

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:353`

## paragon.3160.b.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3160.b.response&lang=en)

### 中文

啊。完全修好可以运作了。我们的物理学家应该会继续进行研究并从中得益。\n\n§H凯德斯一瞬间垂下了目光。§!\n\n但是这很令人惋惜，因为它已经失去了完整的力量。要打开一个稳定的虫洞，另一头必须要有一个目标系统。我先祖的星球上确有一个这样的东西，但不幸的是我们还没有办法复刻出另一个。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:300`

### English

Ah. Fully restored and functional. Our physicists should continue to benefit greatly from the research.\n\n§HKeides drops his gaze momentarily.§!\n\nIt's regrettable, however, that its full power has been lost. To open a stable wormhole, a target system is needed in the system on the receiving end. One such is present on the world of my ancestors. Unfortunately, we do not possess the means to reconstruct another.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:354`

## paragon.3160.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3160.c&lang=en)

### 中文

那现在呢？你要怎么办？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:301`

### English

And now? What will you do?

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:355`

## paragon.3160.c.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3160.c.response&lang=en)

### 中文

我愿为你们效劳。\n\n即便至今科学仍是我最热衷的事情。如果说有什么不同的话，那就祖先的记忆激发了我探索的欲望。星系充满了奇迹，耳听为虚，眼见为实。这是谁都说不定的。有朝一日，我愿意见到我人民的家园从今天的遗迹中重建。\n\n但目前而言，我所学到的一切都归功于你们。我期待用这些东西来惠及我的人民。请问哪里最需要我？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:302`

### English

I am yours to command.\n\nScience remains my passion, even now. If anything, my ancestors' memories have only whetted my appetite for discovery. The galaxy is full of wonder, and there is a difference between knowing and seeing. Who knows? In time I may even see the home of my people restored from the relic it is today.\n\nBut for now, all that I have learned is yours. I look forward to applying the lessons of my people. Where am I most needed?

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:356`

## paragon.3160.d

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.3160.d&lang=en)

### 中文

让科学继续成为你心头的那团火吧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:303`

### English

Let science remain your passion.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:357`

## azaryn_ship_button

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aazaryn_ship_button&lang=en)

### 中文

让一个目标行星开始转变为§Y$pc_gaia$§!。对大部分宜居行星有效，包括§Y$pc_barren$§!。\n\n§R每次使用将消耗一个§Y$NAME_Astrocreator_Azaryn$§!给予的环境改造之核。§!\n改造之核剩余：§Y[fleet.leader.azaryn_nuclei_variable]§!\n\n花费：£energy£§Y10000§!\n时间：£resource_time£§Y3600§!天\n$tt$如果是§Y$pc_barren$§!则为£resource_time£§Y9000§!天

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:343`

### English

Initiates conversion of a targeted planet into a §Y$pc_gaia$§!. Effective on most habitable worlds, including §Y$pc_barren$s§!.\n\n§RConsumes one Terraformation Nucleus from §Y$NAME_Astrocreator_Azaryn$§! per use.§!\nNuclei remaining: §Y[fleet.leader.azaryn_nuclei_variable]§!\n\nCost: £energy£§Y10000§!\nTime: £resource_time£ §Y3600§! days\n$tt$ £resource_time£ §Y9000§! days if §Y$pc_barren$§!

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:403`

## paragon.202.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.202.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§H有人尝试与我们建立通讯。§!\n\n§H我们听到了远处湍急的流水、小型飞禽发出呱噪的叫声又扑棱扑棱地飞走。这份通讯似乎来自一个非常繁茂的生物群系。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:351`

### English

§HSomeone is trying to establish contact with us.§!\n\n§HWe hear the rush of flowing liquid in the distance, and the squawking and wing-flapping of tiny avians taking flight. The transmission seems to be coming from a thriving biome.§!

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:413`

## paragon.206.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.206.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们可以定位这个神秘通讯的源头。通过信号的追踪我们发现了许多不同的坐标点，我们确定这是来自一个移动物体上的通讯。我们的理论认为我们应该是与一艘舰船发生了通讯，一艘可能用于自然保留地之类作用的舰船。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:362`

### English

We were able to locate the source of the mysterious transmissions. On tracing the signals to multiple points of origin, we have ascertained they must be coming from a moving object. Our leading theory suggests we were contacted by a ship, which may be used as a nature reserve of sorts.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:427`

## paragon.206.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.206.a&lang=en)

建立通信。

### English

Establish communications.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:428`

## paragon.208.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.208.name&lang=en)

### 中文

$paragon.206.name$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:364`

### English

$paragon.206.name$

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:430`

## paragon.208.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.208.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§H我们建立了通讯，然后再一次听到了那个生物群系熟悉的声音。然而，这次我们听到了人的说话声，§!\n\n诚挚地向你们问好，[owner.GetAdjective]！我对你们有所期待。原谅我的唐突，但我必须知道你们有多少好奇心。现在让我问你一件事。你们如何看待生命?

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:365`

### English

§HWe establish communication, and are met with the now familiar sounds of the biome. However, this time we hear someone speaking,§!\n\nWarm greetings, [owner.GetAdjective]! I've been expecting you. Pardon the pretense, but I had to know how curious you were. Now let me ask you something. How do you value life?

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:431`

## paragon.208.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.208.a&lang=en)

### 中文

你是什么人？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:366`

### English

Who are you?

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:432`

## paragon.208.a.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.208.a.response&lang=en)

### 中文

啊。你最好还是不要打断寒暄吧？很好，我先介绍一下我自己。\n\n§H在这位植物类生物的面容上绽开了花朵般的微笑。§!\n\n我叫§Y艾莎琳§!。但在你们回答我的问题之前，我不会再说更多了。告诉我吧，你们认为生命有什么价值？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:367`

### English

Ah. You'd rather not snip the pleasantries? Very well. Allow me to introduce myself.\n\n§HA smile spreads across the plantoid's face.§!\n\nI am §YAzaryn§!. But until you answer my question, I will say no more. Now tell me, what value do you ascribe to life?

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:433`

## paragon.208.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.208.b&lang=en)

### 中文

那要看情况。毕竟生命有很多不同的形式。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:368`

### English

That depends. Life comes in many forms.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:434`

## paragon.208.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.208.c&lang=en)

### 中文

这是什么脑筋急转弯吗？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:369`

### English

Is this a trick question?

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:435`

## paragon.208.d

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.208.d&lang=en)

### 中文

请自我阐述。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:370`

### English

Explain yourself.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:436`

## paragon.208.d.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.208.d.response&lang=en)

### 中文

我能理解你不愿回答。但你必须明白，如果我能告诉你你更多信息的话我肯定会说。但我不能冒污染数据的险。再次请问，你们如何看待生命?

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:371`

### English

I understand your reluctance to answer. You must understand, if I could give you more information I would. But I can't risk contaminating the data. Please, how do you value life?

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:437`

## paragon.208.e

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.208.e&lang=en)

### 中文

结束通讯。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:372`

### English

Terminate communications.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:438`

## paragon.210.name

## paragon.210.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.210.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

挺合理的回答。几乎所有人都会毫不犹豫地摘除可能危及自己生命的东西。但是我们要记住，所有的生命都有无限的潜力。\n\n现在，我来解释一下我联系你的原因。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:374`

### English

A sensible answer. Few would hesitate to remove something that would threaten life. Yet, it bears remembering that all life holds infinite potential.\n\nNow, I will shed some light on my reason for contacting you.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:441`

## paragon.210.desc_alt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.210.desc_alt&lang=en)

### 中文

§H这位植物类叹了口气。§!\n\n终于见到了一个脑子里不只想着征服的民族。我也有同感，所有的生命都有无限的潜力。\n\n现在，请允许我解释一下我联系您的原因。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:375`

### English

§HThe plantoid sighs.§!\n\nAt last, a people with more than conquest on their minds. I share your sentiment. All life holds infinite potential.\n\nNow, allow me to explain my reason for contacting you.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:442`

请继续。

### English

Go on.

## paragon.212.desc_with_intro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.212.desc_with_intro&lang=en)

### 中文

我叫§Y艾莎琳§!，是来自艾莎提亚的一位前太空生物学家

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:380`

### English

I am §YAzaryn§!, a former astrobiologist from Azathea

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:448`

## paragon.212.desc_without_intro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.212.desc_without_intro&lang=en)

### 中文

我是一个来自艾莎提亚的前太空生物学家，

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:381`

### English

I am a former astrobiologist from Azathea,

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:449`

## paragon.212.desc_main_chunk

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.212.desc_main_chunk&lang=en)

### 中文

后来成为了太空创生学者。我一直寻找可以创造新生命的材料。太空创生学需要的能量远超过我自己所能产生的。\n\n§H艾莎琳顿了顿，继续说道§!\n\n请相信我，这是物有所值的。但一开始有些困难。我想先送你一份礼物，看看你能否中和它。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:382`

### English

turned scholar of astrocreation. I seek the materials needed to create new life. Astrocreation requires more energy than I can generate on my own.\n\n§HAzaryn pauses before continuing,§!\n\nI assure you, this is a worthwhile pursuit. But first, a challenge. I would like to send you a gift of sorts. See if you are able to neutralize it.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:450`

## paragon.212.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.212.a&lang=en)

### 中文

艾莎提亚是什么？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:383`

### English

What is Azathea?

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:451`

## paragon.212.a.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.212.a.response&lang=en)

### 中文

艾莎提亚是我的家乡。它曾是一颗盖亚星球，是我们的帝国“星植丛”的首都，也是许多不同物种所栖息的家园。可惜，他们都已经不复存在了。\n\n§H艾莎琳的目光游离了。§!\n\n在其鼎盛时期，我们的星球令人无比叹为观止，上面充满了生命和自然之美。我想，这种生物多样性是前无古人后无来者的。但它最终还是分崩离析了。\n\n现在，请小心对待我的礼物。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:384`

### English

Azathea was my home. It was a gaia world, and the capital of the Astrophytan Plexus, my peoples' empire, and home to many different species. Sadly, they are all lost.\n\n§HAzaryn's gaze drifts.§!\n\nAt its pinnacle our world was a wonder to behold, teeming with life and natural beauty. I imagine such biological diversity has scarcely existed since. But it all fell apart.\n\nNow, treat my gift with care.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:452`

## paragon.212.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.212.b&lang=en)

### 中文

太空创生学是什么？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:385`

### English

What is astrocreation?

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:453`

## paragon.212.b.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.212.b.response&lang=en)

### 中文

问的很好。$paragon_astrocreation_is$\n\n很有趣吧？我送给你们的礼物就是向着这个目标的第一步。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:386`

### English

An excellent question. $paragon_astrocreation_is$\n\nAmazing, yes? The gift I send you is a first step toward that end.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:454`

## paragon.212.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.212.c&lang=en)

### 中文

说说你那艘船。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:387`

### English

Tell us about your ship.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:455`

## paragon.212.c.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.212.c.response&lang=en)

### 中文

啊没错，这对不熟悉的人看起来确实太离奇了。没错，这就是我舰船的内部。\n\n§H艾莎琳用手势示意四周。§!\n\n这是“生息之所”。是一艘穹顶舰，其中包含内建的功能完整的生态系统。我承认，对此我非常自豪。\n\n送给你们的礼物就是在这里培育的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:388`

### English

Ah yes, this may seem unusual to the uninitiated. Indeed, this is the interior of my ship.\n\n§HAzaryn gestures to her surroundings.§!\n\nThis is the Vivarium. It is a dome ship, complete with a fully functioning integrated ecosystem. I admit, I am quite proud of it.\n\nThe gift I have given you was grown here.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:456`

## paragon.212.d

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.212.d&lang=en)

### 中文

下次再说。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:389`

### English

Until next time.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:457`

## AZARYNS_BACTERIA_PROJECT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AAZARYNS_BACTERIA_PROJECT&lang=en)

### 中文

艾莎琳的礼物

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:390`

### English

Azaryn's Gift

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:459`

## AZARYNS_BACTERIA_PROJECT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AAZARYNS_BACTERIA_PROJECT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

我们从艾莎琳那里收到的所谓礼物实际上是一种植物性细菌，且与我们所见过的任何细菌都不同。中和它将会是一个危险的挑战。然而，如果我们成功了，它将进一步加深我们对科学和医学的理解。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:391`

### English

The supposed gift we received from Azaryn is in fact a plant-based bacteria, unlike anything we've ever seen. Neutralizing it will provide a dangerous challenge. However, if we are successful, it would further our understanding of science and medicine.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:460`

## paragon.214.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.214.name&lang=en)

### 中文

艾莎琳的菌种

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:392`

### English

Azaryn's Bacteria

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:462`

## paragon.214.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.214.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们对艾莎琳的有机“礼物”取得了一定的进展。跨学科科学进步日益增长的潜力令人鼓舞。\n\n我们整理好了目前为止的发现，消除了该微生物的任何潜在威胁，并准备好再次联系艾莎琳。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:393`

### English

We have made progress with Azaryn's organic 'gift'. The growing potential for interdisciplinary scientific advancement is encouraging.\n\nWe have collated our findings, having neutralized any potential threat from the microorganism, and we are ready to contact Azaryn again.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:463`

## paragon.214.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.214.a&lang=en)

### 中文

继续。开启通讯。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:394`

### English

Proceed. Open communications.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:464`

## paragon.216.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.216.name&lang=en)

### 中文

$NAME_Astrocreator_Azaryn$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:395`

### English

$NAME_Astrocreator_Azaryn$

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:466`

## paragon.216.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.216.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§H艾莎琳听到我们的进展非常开心。§!\n\n太棒了！我非常感激，并为你们的努力喝彩。那么，[owner.GetName]，我还有另一个问题。\n\n你会为了逃避死亡而夺取另一个人的生命吗？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:396`

### English

§HAzaryn is delighted to hear of our breakthrough.§!\n\nWonderful! I appreciate and applaud your efforts. Now, [owner.GetName], I have another question.\n\nTo escape death, would you take the life of another?

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:467`

## paragon.216.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.216.a&lang=en)

### 中文

是，我们会这么做。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:397`

### English

Yes, we would.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:468`

## paragon.216.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.216.b&lang=en)

### 中文

不，我们不会。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:398`

### English

No, we would not.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:469`

## paragon.218.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.218.name&lang=en)

### 中文

$paragon.201.name$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:399`

### English

$paragon.201.name$

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:471`

## paragon.218.desc_yes

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.218.desc_yes&lang=en)

### 中文

确实，大多数人都会这么选。毕竟生存是所有生物的驱动力。理解并接受这个事实也许有一天能拯救你，还有你的良心。\n\n$paragon.218.desc_flower$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:400`

### English

Indeed, most would. Survival is the driving force of all living things, after all. Understanding and accepting that fact might save you one day, and your conscience.\n\n$paragon.218.desc_flower$

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:472`

## paragon.218.desc_no

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.218.desc_no&lang=en)

### 中文

真的，你们真的不会吗？\n\n§H艾莎琳挠了挠头。§!\n\n我觉得这让人有点沮丧，也不可信。虽然我觉得这在某种程度上是令人钦佩的。要是这是真的就好了。\n\n$paragon.218.desc_flower$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:401`

### English

Truly, you would not?\n\n§HAzaryn scratches her head.§!\n\nI find that discouraging, and rather unconvincing. Although I suppose it's admirable in a way. If only it were true.\n\n$paragon.218.desc_flower$

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:473`

## paragon.218.desc_flower

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.218.desc_flower&lang=en)

### 中文

在我的下一个请求之前，我还有一件礼物要给你们。这是艾莎提亚大王花，是一种我的舰船上原生的花卉。我给你的挑战是让它开花。暂时再会了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:402`

### English

Before my next query, I have another gift for you. It is a Rafflesia Azathea, a type of flower, native to my ship. I challenge you to make it bloom. Farewell, for now.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:474`

## paragon.218.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.218.a&lang=en)

### 中文

很好。再会吧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:403`

### English

Very well. Goodbye.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:475`

## AZARYNS_RAFFLESIA_PROJECT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AAZARYNS_RAFFLESIA_PROJECT&lang=en)

### 中文

艾莎琳的大王花

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:404`

### English

Azaryn's Rafflesia

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:477`

## AZARYNS_RAFFLESIA_PROJECT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AAZARYNS_RAFFLESIA_PROJECT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

艾莎琳给我们一个任务，让我们尝试让一朵在她船上生态系统原生的巨大的花苞开花。基于初步的观测，这朵花可能对太空生物学有着极为重要的意义。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:405`

### English

Azaryn has tasked us with blooming a giant flower bud, native to her dome ship's ecosystem. Based on initial observations, this flower may be of great astrobiological significance.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:478`

## paragon.220.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.220.name&lang=en)

### 中文

艾莎琳的大王花

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:406`

### English

Azaryn's Rafflesia

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:480`

## paragon.220.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.220.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们最初试图引起花苞的反应，但效果并不好。只有当使用超出通常安全参数的电刺激时，这朵花才能开放。我们已经完成了艾莎琳的最新任务，并准备再次与她联系。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:407`

### English

Our initial attempts to provoke a reaction from the flower bud were underwhelming. It was only when we applied electrical stimuli beyond our usual safety parameters that we were able to bring the flower to bloom. We have completed Azaryn's latest task, and are ready to contact her again.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:481`

## paragon.220.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.220.a&lang=en)

### 中文

做得好。联系艾莎琳吧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:408`

### English

Well done. Contact Azaryn.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:482`

## paragon.222.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.222.name&lang=en)

### 中文

$NAME_Astrocreator_Azaryn$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:409`

### English

$NAME_Astrocreator_Azaryn$

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:484`

## paragon.222.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.222.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§H一只小巧的授粉无人机在艾莎琳的脑边嗡嗡作响。她温柔地把它挥开。§!\n\n再次问好。艾莎提亚大王花真是一种有趣的植物，你们也赞同吧？进化真是一件奇妙的事情。我对于你们能对其施加合适量的能量来使其盛开感到印象深刻。\n\n§H她兴奋地拍起了手。§!\n\n又到了提问时间。在规模都很大的前提下，你们会为了拯救其他的生命而牺牲自己的同类吗？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:410`

### English

§HA tiny pollination drone buzzes around Azaryn's head. She gently wafts it away.§!\n\nHello again. The Rafflesia Azathea sure is an interesting plant, wouldn't you agree? Evolution truly is a wondrous thing. I'm impressed you were able to apply the required amount of energy to make it bloom.\n\n§HShe claps her hands excitedly.§!\n\nTime for another question. Would you sacrifice your own kind if it meant you could save lives elsewhere, on a massive scale?

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:485`

### English

$paragon.201.name$

## paragon.224.desc_yes

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.224.desc_yes&lang=en)

### 中文

§H艾莎琳心领神会地点了点头。§!\n\n是的，也许这个答案很明显，但在那个场景之下，可能很难看到正确答案。我们必须抛下自己的情感，做出那些顾全大局的事情。\n\n$paragon.224.desc_question$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:414`

### English

§HAzaryn nods in understanding.§!\n\nYes, it may seem the obvious choice but, when caught up in the moment, it can be very hard to see it through. We must set aside our emotions and do what is best for the greater good.\n\n$paragon.224.desc_question$

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:490`

## paragon.224.desc_no

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.224.desc_no&lang=en)

### 中文

§H艾莎琳同情地点了点头。§!\n\n确实，这不是一件容易的事。我赞许你们的诚实。\n\n然而我相信，为了顾全大局，智慧生命必须给那些无私的选择以更高的优先级。\n\n$paragon.224.desc_question$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:415`

### English

§HAzaryn nods sympathetically.§!\n\nIndeed, it is not an easy thing to do. I appreciate your honesty.\n\nHowever, it is my belief that for the betterment of all, intelligent life must always give precedence to the selfless choice.\n\n$paragon.224.desc_question$

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:491`

## paragon.224.desc_question

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.224.desc_question&lang=en)

### 中文

我再问你们最后一个问题。\n\n你们在消灭威胁的时候会有任何迟疑吗？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:416`

### English

I have one final question for you.\n\nWould you hesitate to remove a threat?

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:492`

## paragon.224.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.224.a&lang=en)

### 中文

没错。决定性的行为确实需要三思。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:417`

### English

Yes. Decisive actions require consideration.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:493`

## paragon.224.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.224.b&lang=en)

### 中文

不。犹豫就会死。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:418`

### English

No. Hesitation gets you killed.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:494`

## paragon.226.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.226.name&lang=en)

### 中文

$paragon.201.name$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:419`

### English

$paragon.201.name$

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:496`

## paragon.226.desc_yes

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.226.desc_yes&lang=en)

### 中文

我同意。果断很重要，但有些情况需要额外考虑。有时候你并不总是那么幸运，能够马上获得所有必要的信息。耐心对你大有好处。\n\n$paragon.226.desc_end$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:420`

### English

I agree. Decisiveness is vital, but some situations require extra consideration. Sometimes you are not fortunate enough to have all of the necessary information right away. Patience could benefit you greatly.\n\n$paragon.226.desc_end$

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:497`

## paragon.226.desc_no

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.226.desc_no&lang=en)

### 中文

你喜欢有时间思考。不幸的是，有时间思考可是一种特权。决定是一定有做出的，而片刻的深思熟虑可能是成功与失败的区别。\n\n$paragon.226.desc_end$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:421`

### English

Ah, you like having time to think. Unfortunately, one does not always have the privilege of extra time. Decisions must be made, and a moment of deliberation could be the difference between success and failure.\n\n$paragon.226.desc_end$

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:498`

## paragon.226.desc_end

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.226.desc_end&lang=en)

### 中文

§H艾莎琳沉默片刻，然后给出了结论：§!\n\n很好，我已经问了你们够多问题了。你们给予了我非常多的耐心。我会花一点时间收集这些信息。我们很快会再一次谈话的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:422`

### English

§HAzaryn is quiet for a moment. Then concludes:§!\n\nWell, I have asked you enough questions. You possess admirable patience. I will take some time to collate this information. We shall speak again soon.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:499`

## paragon.228.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.228.name&lang=en)

### 中文

$NAME_Astrocreator_Azaryn$

### English

$NAME_Astrocreator_Azaryn$

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:501`

## paragon.228.desc_opening_worst

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.228.desc_opening_worst&lang=en)

### 中文

你好！你们已经向我展示了你们的实力。我希望能适时教会你们利用我的太空创生之力。\n\n§H艾莎琳鞠了一躬。§!\n\n我们会携手让星系再度变得郁郁葱葱。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:424`

### English

Hello again! You have proven your potential. In time I hope to teach you to utilize my astrocreation powers.\n\n§HAzaryn bows.§!\n\nTogether, we will reinvigorate the galaxy.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:502`

## paragon.228.desc_opening_mid

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.228.desc_opening_mid&lang=en)

### 中文

你好，[owner.GetAdjective]。很高兴再次见到你们。\n\n尽管我们有许多的不同，但我希望你们能记得我的教导，并学会用创生学的知识造福星系。\n\n

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:425`

### English

Hello, [owner.GetAdjective]. I am pleased to see you again.\n\nDespite our differences, it is my hope that you will embrace my teaching, and learn to utilize astrocreation for the good of the galaxy.\n\n

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:503`

## paragon.228.desc_opening_best

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.228.desc_opening_best&lang=en)

### 中文

啊，[owner.GetAdjective]。诚挚地向你们问好。我的结论是，你们是太空创生学杰出的继承人！\n\n§H艾莎琳微笑着。§!\n\n我们将携手将生命播撒向虚空。我们将携手为所有人带去希望！\n\n

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:426`

### English

Ah, [owner.GetAdjective]. Warm greetings. I have concluded that you are an excellent advocate for astrocreation!\n\n§HAzaryn smiles.§!\n\nTogether we shall breathe life into the void. Together, we shall inspire hope for all!\n\n

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:504`

## paragon.228.desc_end

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.228.desc_end&lang=en)

### 中文

只要你努力创造新生命，我的技能和我的船愿为你所用。如果你喜欢，我很乐意加入你。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:430`

### English

As long as you strive to create new life, my skills and my ship are at your disposal. If this pleases you, I will gladly join you.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:508`

## paragon.228.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.228.a&lang=en)

### 中文

所以太空创生学到底是什么？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:431`

### English

What exactly is astrocreation?

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:509`

## paragon.228.a.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.228.a.response&lang=en)

### 中文

对哦，请原谅我。我太激动了，差点忘了解释最重要的细节。但也许最好进行一个展示以方便理解……\n\n$paragon_astrocreation_is$\n\n是的，很快你将亲自见证一次太空创生。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:432`

### English

Ah yes, forgive me. In my excitement, I have neglected to explain this most important detail. But perhaps a demonstration would be best...\n\n$paragon_astrocreation_is$\n\nYes, soon you may witness astrocreation for yourself.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:510`

## paragon_astrocreation_is

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon_astrocreation_is&lang=en)

### 中文

太空创生是一种高等级的环境改造。它是如此地强大。在一颗行星上播种一颗我的环境改造之核——§H艾莎琳指了指她背后的花苞§!——我们就能将几乎任何一种行星改造成一颗欣欣向荣的盖亚星球。这个过程需要许多能量，但结果是非常壮观的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:433`

### English

Astrocreation is an advanced variant of terraformation. It is incredibly powerful. By seeding a planet with one of my Terraformation Nuclei--§HAzaryn indicates the buds on her back§!--we will be able to transform almost any planet into a thriving Gaia World. The process requires a great deal of energy, but the results are nothing short of spectacular.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:511`

## paragon.228.b.custom_tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.228.b.custom_tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

§H$NAME_Astrocreator_Azaryn$§!作为一位§Y领袖§!加入了我们帝国。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:434`

### English

§Y$NAME_Astrocreator_Azaryn$§! joins our Empire as a §HLeader§!.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:513`

## paragon.228.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.228.c&lang=en)

### 中文

你背上的那些是什么？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:435`

### English

What are those things on your back?

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:515`

## paragon.228.c.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.228.c.response&lang=en)

### 中文

§H艾莎琳似乎对这个问题很惊讶。§!\n\n简言之，这是我们物种独有的一种特质。在我们年轻的时候，它们会快速生长——我必须补充一下，会有点儿不舒服——并且需要定期修剪，不然它就会把我们自己压垮。§H她微笑着。§!但是啊，等到了我这个年纪，这种小烦恼就是遥远的怀念了。\n\n它们其实并不是种子，但能发挥相似的作用。我将其命名为环境改造之核。其中沉睡着成长、重生和生命的潜力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:436`

### English

§HAzaryn appears surprised at the question.§!\n\nA trait unique to my species, simply put. In our youth, they grow quickly - not without discomfort, I might add - and must be pruned regularly, lest we be weighed down by them. §HShe smiles.§! At my age, however, such troubles are a thing of the past.\n\nThey are not quite seeds, but it would do for an analogy. I've come to call them Terraformation Nuclei. Within them sleeps the potential for growth, for rebirth, and for life.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:516`

## paragon.228.d

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.228.d&lang=en)

### 中文

你那台嗡嗡作响的小无人机是什么？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:437`

### English

What is that buzzing, tiny drone of yours?

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:517`

## paragon.228.d.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.228.d.response&lang=en)

### 中文

啊，你说这个？§H艾莎琳温柔地抱起那只小小的飞虫。§!这是一只授粉无人机。它们能确保我飞船上生态系统生机勃勃，生生不息。\n\n它们并不是完全无机的，甚至还有自行产生花粉的能力。§H艾莎琳指了指无人机的尾部。§!它们甚至可以利用电池末端的静电来调节花粉的传递。很可爱吧？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:438`

### English

Ah, this one? §HAzaryn gently lifts the little flier.§! This is a pollination drone. They help to ensure the vigor and future of my ship's ecosystem.\n\nThey're not altogether inorganic, and even possess the ability to produce pollen on their own. §HAzaryn points at the drone's bottom.§! They can even regulate pollen-transferral by use of static magnetism from their battery terminals. Adorable, isn't it?

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:518`

## paragon.230.desc_main

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.230.desc_main&lang=en)

### 中文

§H艾莎琳正在控制台上飞快地输入着，调节着一些仪器。§!\n\n太棒了！我定位了你的首都开始导航，很快就能到你们那儿。\n\n欧我差点忘了，我舰船的生态系统也是许多外激素的家。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:443`

### English

§HTyping quickly into a command console, Azaryn adjusts some instruments.§!\n\nWonderful! I have plotted a course for your capital, and will be arriving shortly.\n\nOh, and before I forget, my ship's ecosystem is home to a wide variety of ectohormones.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:525`

## paragon.230.worst

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.230.worst&lang=en)

### 中文

感觉我给你这些§Y信息素§!应该挺合适的。它们与你们的物种可以兼容，一定能提供一些正面效果的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:444`

### English

It seems appropriate I give you these §Ypheromones§!. They are compatible with your species, and are sure to provide some positive effects.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:526`

## paragon.230.mid

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.230.mid&lang=en)

### 中文

为了纪念我们的合作，我准备了一份§Y信息素§!作为礼物。这不是我最杰出的工作，但还是会为你们的人民带来一些正面的效果。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:445`

### English

To commemorate our collaboration, I have prepared a gift of §Ypheromones§!. It's not my best work, but they will provide some positive effects to your people.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:527`

## paragon.230.best

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.230.best&lang=en)

### 中文

为了纪念此刻的愉悦，请接收这份§Y信息素§!作为礼物。里面包含了一些可以为你的人民带去好处的化合物。很值得一用。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:446`

### English

To mark this joyous occasion, please accept this gift of §Ypheromones§!. They contain a potent compound which will bring certain benefits to your people. It is well deserved.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:528`

## paragon.235.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.235.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们现在确信§Y$NAME_Astrocreator_Azaryn$§!的健康与环境改造之核密不可分。每用掉一个核心，她的健康状况就变糟糕一分，我们猜测一旦用掉最后一个核，她将会死去。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:464`

### English

We now know for certain that §Y$NAME_Astrocreator_Azaryn$§!'s health is inherently tied to her Terraformation Nuclei. Her condition worsened after each use, and we now suspect that upon seeding her final Nuclei, she will die.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:549`

## paragon.235.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.235.a&lang=en)

### English

Regrettable.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:550`

最后的花蕾

### English

The Last Bud

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:552`

## paragon.240.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.240.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§H$NAME_Astrocreator_Azaryn$还在。她舰船上日常的叽叽喳喳变成了一种低沉的哼鸣。她用一种暗哑的声音低语。§!\n\n我并不悲伤。那些星球会欣欣向荣的，我很荣幸献出了自己的生命。谢谢你们，朋友。\n\n§H她走了。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:467`

### English

§H$NAME_Astrocreator_Azaryn$ is still. The usual cacophony of sound from her ship has fallen to a low hum. She speaks in a strained whisper.§!\n\nI am not sad. So worlds may thrive, I have gladly given my life. Thank you, friends.\n\n§HShe is gone.§!

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:553`

## paragon.240.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.240.a&lang=en)

### 中文

巨大的损失。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:468`

### English

A great loss.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:555`

### English

## paragon_withered_dome_ship_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon_withered_dome_ship_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在§Y$NAME_Astrocreator_Azaryn$§!死后，她的穹顶舰所残余之物。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:470`

### English

The remains of §Y$NAME_Astrocreator_Azaryn$§!'s dome ship, left behind after her death.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:557`

## paragon.241.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.241.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§H我们收到了一份预先录制的信息。是来自艾莎琳的。§!\n\n虽然我接纳了自己的牺牲，但我无法接受生息之所以及船上的万物和我一起死去的这件事。这艘舰船与我紧密相连，当我枯萎时，它也会枯萎。\n\n我对此有所预见，所以生息之所是以模块化设计的，其中的每个生态系统都能够自我维持。准备工作已经都提前做好了，你们只需要找到一颗行星重新把栖息地安置下来。我相信你们能完成这项任务。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:471`

### English

§HWe receive a pre-recorded message. It is from Azaryn.§!\n\nAlthough I accept my sacrifice, I cannot stand the thought of the Vivarium and the lifeforms on board dying with me. The ship is profoundly linked to me, and when I wither, it will too.\n\nIn anticipation of this, I designed the Vivarium in a modular fashion, with each ecosystem capable of sustaining itself. Preparations have been made, and all you need to do is find a planet to rehome the habitat. I trust you to complete this task.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:559`

## paragon.241.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.241.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们会的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:472`

### English

We will.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:560`

## paragon.241.a.custom_tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.241.a.custom_tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

艾莎琳的§Y$building_contained_ecosphere$§!选址重建的准备工作已经完成。可以重建在一颗我们选定的行星上。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:473`

### English

Preparations for the relocation and reconstruction of Azaryn's §Y$building_contained_ecosphere$§! have been made. It may be reconstructed on a planet of our choosing.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:561`

## building_contained_ecosphere_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Abuilding_contained_ecosphere_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y$NAME_Astrocreator_Azaryn$§!的遗产，是在她去世时遗留给我们的。它只是生息之所内建生态系统的一小部分。\n\n向该行星的引入高浓度的§H$contained_ecosphere_pheromones$§!，对人口施加正面影响。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:475`

### English

§Y$NAME_Astrocreator_Azaryn$§!'s legacy, left to us when she passed. It is but a small section of the Vivarium's internal ecosystem.\n\nIntroduces a high concentration of §H$contained_ecosphere_pheromones$§! to a planet's atmosphere, applying positive effects to the population.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:564`

Beneficial pheromones regularly released by §H$building_contained_ecosphere$§!, applying positive effects to the population.

## paragon.260.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.260.name&lang=en)

核心样本

Nucleus Sample

## paragon.260.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.260.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的一位科学家自行从§Y$NAME_Astrocreator_Azaryn$§!的环境改造之核上悄悄取下的一份样本。\n\n对该花苞的研究可以提供一些现阶段艾莎琳还没打算分享的有价值的信息。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:479`

### English

Acting of their own volition, one of our scientists has covertly obtained a sample from one of §Y$NAME_Astrocreator_Azaryn$§!'s Terraformation Nuclei.\n\nResearching this bud could provide valuable information which Azaryn does not seem inclined to share at this stage.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:569`

## paragon.260.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.260.a&lang=en)

### 中文

着手研究。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:480`

### English

Commence research.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:570`

## paragon.260.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.260.b&lang=en)

把样本还给艾莎琳。

### English

Return the sample to Azaryn.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:571`

## paragon.261.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.261.name&lang=en)

### 中文

核心样本归还

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:482`

### English

Nucleus Sample Returned

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:573`

## paragon.261.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.261.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§H$NAME_Astrocreator_Azaryn$松了口气。§!\n\n你们能决定将这个还给我，我的感激之情无法言表。如果不能正确控制这种力量，结果可能是灾难性的。\n\n值得庆幸的是，历史不会重演。为了奖励你的诚实，我会准备一批新的费洛蒙。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:483`

### English

§H$NAME_Astrocreator_Azaryn$ is relieved.§!\n\nI cannot understate my gratitude that you have seen fit to return this. Without the proper means to control this power, the results could have been catastrophic.\n\nThankfully, history will not repeat itself. To reward your honesty, I will prepare a fresh batch of pheromones.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:574`

## paragon.261.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.261.a&lang=en)

### 中文

不会再发生这种事了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:484`

### English

It will not happen again.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:575`

## paragon.261.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.261.b&lang=en)

### 中文

历史重演？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:485`

### English

History repeating itself?

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:576`

## paragon.261.b.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.261.b.response&lang=en)

### 中文

我多言了。\n\n§H艾莎琳摇了摇头。§!\n\n干预自然会产生可怕的后果。在年轻的时候我技能尚不成熟，因此犯了一个错误……\n\n我们的星球曾经欣欣向荣，美丽非凡。但一个小小的错误计算导致了一种意想不到的细菌的入侵。生态平衡被打破，随之而来的便是大灭绝。我们为自己的粗心大意付出了代价。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:486`

### English

I've said too much.\n\n§HAzaryn shakes her head.§!\n\nMeddling with nature can have terrible consequences. In my younger days, before I honed my skills, I made a mistake...\n\nOur planets were flourishing. They were beautiful. But a simple miscalculation led to the introduction of an unforeseen bacteria. The balance was tipped, and mass extinction followed. We were not cautious, and we paid the price.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:577`

## NUCLEUS_SAMPLE_STOLEN

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ANUCLEUS_SAMPLE_STOLEN&lang=en)

### 中文

环境改造之核研究

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:487`

### English

Terraformation Nucleus Research

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:579`

## NUCLEUS_SAMPLE_STOLEN_DESC

## paragon.265.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.265.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的秘密科学团队通过研究§Y$NAME_Astrocreator_Azaryn$§!的核心样本中学到很多东西。这个计划完成了，而艾莎琳对此毫不知情。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:490`

### English

Our covert science team has been able to learn a lot from studying the sample of §Y$NAME_Astrocreator_Azaryn$§!'s nucleus. This project has concluded successfully, and Azaryn is none the wiser.

## paragon.270.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.270.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[event_target:nucleus_building_planet.GetName]上的设施遭受到了严重的结构破坏，被快速增殖的制备摧垮。研究环境改造之核样本的科学家无法控制局面了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:492`

### English

The facility on [event_target:nucleus_building_planet.GetName] has suffered substantial structural damage and is overrun with rapidly growing vegetation. The scientists studying the Terraformation Nuclei sample cannot control the situation.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:586`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.270.a&lang=en)

$NAME_Astrocreator_Azaryn$

## paragon.272.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.272.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§H艾莎琳有点惊讶。§!\n\n欧，嗨！很高兴见到你！\n\n不得不承认，我们合作带来的好处比我预想的要多得多。我们正在一起大步迈进。这样的事情只有我们互相信任才能实现。\n\n§H她亲切地微笑着。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:497`

### English

§HAzaryn is surprised.§!\n\n Oh, hello. Good to see you!\n\nI must say this partnership is proving a greater boon than I would have imagined. We are making great strides together. Such things are only possible with trust.\n\n§HShe smiles warmly.§!

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:592`

## paragon.272.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.272.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们有事情想坦白。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:498`

### English

We need to tell you something.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:593`

## paragon.273.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.273.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我知道了。\n\n§H她叹了口气。§!\n\n行吧，损失已经发生了。幸好你们这么快就来联络我了。我们必须迅速行动。这需要你方大量的投入。有多少就拿出来多少，这样我们可以尽快结束掉这个问题。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:499`

### English

I see.\n\n§HShe sighs.§!\n\nWell, the damage is done. It is lucky you contacted me sooner rather than later. We must act quickly. This will require a significant investment on your part. Give me what you can so we may end this.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:595`

## paragon.273.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.273.a&lang=en)

请拿去吧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:500`

### English

Take it all.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:597`

### English

## paragon.275.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.275.name&lang=en)

### 中文

绿色恐怖

### English

Green Terror

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:600`

## paragon.275.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.275.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们研究环境改造之核样本的研究设施被毁了。由于一直无法阻止入侵性植物的蔓延，现在，新盛开的植物群正在释放出能引起皮肤起泡的花粉，这种花粉一旦吸入后果是致命的。这让[event_target:nucleus_building_planet.GetName]危在旦夕。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:503`

### English

The research facility where we were studying the Terraformation Nuclei sample has been devastated. We have been unable to stop the spread of invasive plant life. Now, the freshly bloomed flora are emitting skin-blistering pollen, which is highly toxic if inhaled. This puts [event_target:nucleus_building_planet.GetName] at risk.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:601`

### English

$NAME_Astrocreator_Azaryn$

## paragon.280.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.280.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§H$NAME_Astrocreator_Azaryn$非常地沮丧。§!\n\n我早知道了你们的贪婪，但我没想到你们竟还如此狂妄，甚至将自己人民的安危置于不顾。他们已经岌岌可危了！\n\n我正在为这个过度增生准备应对措施。但是因为你的玩忽职守造成的伤者需要紧急治疗。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:508`

### English

§H$NAME_Astrocreator_Azaryn$ is crestfallen.§!\n\nI knew you were greedy, but I did not expect you to be so proud that you would risk hurting your own people. They are dying!\n\nI am preparing countermeasures to end this rampant growth. But the victims of your negligence will require immediate medical treatment.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:608`

## paragon.550.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.550.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们伟大的§Y[From.GetTitle][From.GetName]§!为国鞠躬尽瘁，享年[From.GetAge]岁。人们高呼，为了纪念这位领袖对§Y[Root.GetName]§!的贡献，要举办一场盛大的追思仪式。这一盛事能将民众团结在一起，巩固我们的理念。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:514`

### English

Our great §Y[From.GetTitle] [From.GetName]§! has passed away at the age of [From.GetAge] while in service to our empire. The people clamor for a memorial service to honor this leader's contribution to the §Y[Root.GetName]§!. A grand event will give everyone closure, and bolster our ideals.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:616`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.551.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在长长的游行队伍中，§Y[From.GetName]§!静静地沉眠，§Y[Root.GetName]§!的每一位公民都受邀前来表示敬意。\n\n葬礼之后是为期一周的盛大祭典，祭典花费巨大。§Y[From.GetName]§!的透明水晶棺材在§Y[Root.Capital.GetName]§!纪念堂展出。人群排在街头，想要一睹他们伟大领袖的最终安息之处。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:534`

### English

§Y[From.GetName]§! has been laid to rest following a public procession. Every citizen of §Y[Root.GetName]§! was invited to pay their respects.\n\nThe funeral is followed by a week-long festival, with no expenses spared. §Y[From.GetName]§!'s translucent crystal casket is displayed at the §Y[Root.Capital.GetName]§! memorial site, where the crowds line the streets hoping to catch a glimpse of the great leader in their final resting place.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:638`

## paragon.551.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.551.a&lang=en)

### 中文

一场恰当的送别仪式。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:535`

### English

A fitting send-off.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:639`

## paragon.552.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.552.name&lang=en)

### 中文

私密葬礼

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:536`

### English

A Private Funeral

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:641`

## paragon.552.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.552.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[From.GetName]§!在最亲近的亲属和同事的环绕中静静地沉眠。其对§Y[Root.GetName]§!的贡献不菲，但现在并不是能铺张浪费的时候。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:537`

### English

§Y[From.GetName]§! is laid to rest in a private ceremony with their closest family members and colleagues. Their contribution to the §Y[Root.GetName]§! was an important one, but now is not the time for exorbitant expenditure.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:642`

## paragon.552.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.552.a&lang=en)

### 中文

一场合适的告别仪式。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:538`

### English

An appropriate farewell.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:643`

## paragon.553.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.553.name&lang=en)

### 中文

即将到来的死亡

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:539`

### English

An Awaited Death

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:645`

## paragon.553.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.553.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们对§Y[From.GetTitle]§!§Y[From.GetName]§!的谨慎处置被媒体迅速捕捉到了。尽管一些人表达了担忧，但许多消息来源还是坚定地指出了这位领袖的缺点。当前的普遍看法是，我们终于可以找一位更合适的继任者来替代现在这位领袖了。\n\n

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:540`

### English

The discreet disposal of our §Y[From.GetTitle]§!, §Y[From.GetName]§!, has been promptly noted by the press. While others express concern, many sources adamantly point out the leader's shortcomings. The general sentiment is relief that we can now replace this leader with a more fitting successor.\n\n

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:646`

## paragon.553.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.553.a&lang=en)

### 中文

是时候向前看了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:541`

### English

It is time to move on.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:647`

## paragon.554.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.554.name&lang=en)

### 中文

生不带来，死不带走

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:542`

### English

From Matter to Matter

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:649`

## paragon.554.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.554.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在一场简短的追悼会后，[From.GetTitle]的遗体被捐赠给了科学界，以对基础医药研究和教育继续做贡献。\n\n这些过程中剩下的东西最终将被水火化，变成富含矿物质的肥料，确保身体的任何部分都不会被浪费。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:543`

### English

After a brief commemoration, the [From.GetTitle]'s body has been donated to science to contribute towards essential medical research and education.\n\nWhat is left from these procedures will in the end be water cremated and turned into a mineral-rich fertilizer, ensuring no part of the body goes to waste.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:650`

## paragon.554.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.554.a&lang=en)

### 中文

这就是生死的循环。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:544`

### English

Such is the cycle of life.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:651`

## paragon.555.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.555.name&lang=en)

### 中文

悲伤的告别

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:545`

### English

A Somber Departure

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:653`

## paragon.555.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.555.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

人们聚集在一起，追悼§Y[From.GetName]§!的一生及其丰功伟绩。人们挤满了街道，向东望不见头，向西望不见尾。颂歌一直唱到了夜幕降临。[From.GetHeSheCap]现在已经跨入彼岸。\n\n[From.GetHisHer]的布道节选现在在整个帝国回荡，提醒着我们共同的目标。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:546`

### English

The people gathered in their masses to celebrate the life and career of §Y[From.GetName]§!. Huge processions filled the streets, and hymns were sung long into the night. [From.GetHeSheCap] is now waiting for us on the other side of the veil.\n\nPopular excerpts from [From.GetHisHer] sermons are now reverberating across the empire, reminding us of our common goal.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:654`

## paragon.555.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.555.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们终将再会。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:547`

### English

Until we meet again.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:655`

## paragon.556.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.556.name&lang=en)

### 中文

庄严卓著的送别

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:548`

### English

A Distinguished Farewell

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:657`

## paragon.556.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.556.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

大型阅兵方阵和舰队护卫着§Y[From.GetName]§!的灵柩前往最终安息之地。[From.GetHisHerCap]的遗体被发射去了最近的恒星，并全程向全社会广播。\n\n逝世领袖的个人战略部署和日志成为了下一批军校学员课程中的内容。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:549`

### English

A huge parade of troops and a flyover by naval forces marked the arrival of §Y[From.GetName]§!'s casket to its final resting place. [From.GetHisHerCap] body was fired into the nearest star during a society-wide broadcast.\n\nThe departed leader's personal strategies and log books will become a part of the curriculum for the next generation of cadets.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:658`

## paragon.556.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.556.a&lang=en)

### 中文

值得的送别。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:550`

### English

A worthy sendoff.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:659`

## paragon.557.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.557.name&lang=en)

### 中文

基金

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:551`

### English

Foundation

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:661`

## paragon.557.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.557.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

为了纪念刚刚去世的伟大§Y[From.GetTitle]§!，§Y[From.GetName]§!，我们设立了一个非盈利的基金会。这个基金会标志着星际的和平与和谐发展迈出了一步，包容了不同社群和物种之间的分歧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:552`

### English

A non-profit foundation has been established to honor the passing of the great §Y[From.GetTitle]§!, §Y[From.GetName]§!. The foundation marks a step towards peace and interstellar harmony, bridging differences between communities and species.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:662`

## paragon.557.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.557.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们共同创造了一项遗产。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:553`

### English

A legacy has been created.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:663`

## paragon.558.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.558.name&lang=en)

### 中文

传统的葬礼

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:554`

### English

A Traditional Funeral

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:665`

## paragon.558.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.558.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

当§Y[From.GetName]§!去世的消息传遍更远的地区时，一种深深的悲痛笼罩了整个帝国。无数的哀悼者遵循传统的葬礼仪式加入了§Y[Root.Capital.GetName]§!的葬礼队伍中。\n\n其遗体被一丝不苟地清洁并进行了适当的装饰。在§Y[From.GetTitle]§!的遗体进行了安葬之前，我们全国上下都要默哀，表示尊重。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:555`

### English

A deep sorrow has gripped the public as news regarding the passing of §Y[From.GetName]§! spreads to the wider community. Countless mourners join the funeral procession on §Y[Root.Capital.GetName]§! to follow the traditional funeral rites.\n\nThe body is meticulously cleansed and appropriately decorated. Before the deceased §Y[From.GetTitle]§! is laid to rest, a moment of silence is observed throughout our systems as a mark of respect.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:666`

## paragon.558.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.558.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们皆是如此。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:556`

### English

This is our way.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:667`

## paragon.559.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.559.name&lang=en)

### 中文

静默远航

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:557`

### English

Silent Voyage

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:669`

## paragon.559.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.559.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

为纪念§Y[From.GetName]§!一生的丰功伟绩，我们为[From.GetHimHer]准备了一场穿越星海的最后航行。\n\n一个容纳着[From.GetTitle]遗体的胶囊被庄严地发射向未探索的宇域，伴随他的还有诗歌、图表、录音和其他贵宾带来的随葬品。将来有一天，[From.GetTitle]的船只可能会在那里找到一个新家，完成[From.GetHisHer]的最后任务。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:558`

### English

The remarkable deeds of §Y[From.GetName]§! have warranted [From.GetHimHer] one last voyage through space.\n\nA capsule containing the [From.GetTitle]'s remains is ceremonially launched into the void alongside poems, charts, recordings, and other memorabilia brought by various dignitaries. One day the [From.GetTitle]'s vessel may find a new home out there, completing [From.GetHisHer] final mission.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:670`

## paragon.559.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.559.a&lang=en)

### 中文

去往繁星之间吧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:559`

### English

Go now, to the stars.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:671`

## paragon.560.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.560.name&lang=en)

### 中文

高耸的塑像

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:560`

### English

A Towering Figure

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:673`

## paragon.560.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.560.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

工匠们获得最好的材料来制作§Y[From.GetName]§!的肖像，将其加入到§Y[Root.GetName]§!传奇英雄的行列中。其庄严的面孔被蚀刻在石头和金属上，让人们牢记这位强大领袖的丰功伟绩。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:561`

### English

Artisans have been provided with the finest materials to fashion the likeness of §Y[From.GetName]§!, adding them to the long line of storied heroes of the §Y[Root.GetName]§!. Their majestic visage is etched in stone and metal, acting as a reminder of what strong leaders can accomplish.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:674`

## paragon.560.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.560.a&lang=en)

### 中文

您的伟大将被永远铭记。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:562`

### English

Your strength will be remembered.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:675`

## paragon.561.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.561.name&lang=en)

### 中文

铭记

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:563`

### English

Name on the Wall

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:677`

## paragon.561.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.561.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[From.GetName]§!的名字与头衔被镌刻在了一座高大耸立的纪念碑上，与无数其他为§Y[Root.GetName]§!的人民鞠躬尽瘁的人们并列。这位备受尊敬的§Y[From.GetTitle]§!将被人民永远铭记，他的贡献为未来数代领袖们开拓道路。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:564`

### English

§Y[From.GetName]§!'s name and title are carved on a towering monument alongside others who have served the §Y[Root.GetName]§! for the good of its population. The respected §Y[From.GetTitle]§! will be fondly remembered by the people, paving the way for future generations of leaders.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:678`

## paragon.561.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.561.a&lang=en)

### 中文

为了大众的福祉。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:565`

### English

For the good of the many.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:679`

## paragon.562.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.562.name&lang=en)

### 中文

地标

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:566`

### English

Landmark

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:681`

## paragon.562.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.562.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这位领袖的去世引起了星际证交所短暂但剧烈的震荡。\n\n除了举办奢华的葬礼，§Y[Root.GetName]§!还将一座较高的摩天楼重命名为[From.GetName]大厦以确保以后这位[From.GetTitle]的名字会被写入公司的编年史中。每间会议室的墙上都挂着一幅经过艺术诠释的肖像，以纪念其对帝国的杰出贡献。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:567`

### English

News of the leader's passing is met with a sharp, but brief downturn in share value at the galactic stock markets.\n\nIn addition to sponsoring the lavish funeral, §Y[Root.GetName]§! rebrands one of its taller skyscrapers as the [From.GetName] Building to ensure that the late [From.GetTitle]'s name is preserved in the annals of the corporate history. An artistically interpreted portrait is also hung on every boardroom wall to commemorate their outstanding contributions to the empire.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:682`

## paragon.562.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.562.a&lang=en)

### 中文

剪彩吧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:568`

### English

Cut the ribbon.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:683`

## paragon.563.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.563.name&lang=en)

### 中文

新生命

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:569`

### English

New Life

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:685`

## paragon.563.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.563.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[From.Species.GetName]§!的去世并不像表面上看起来那么彻底。尽管躯干已经开始劣化，但核心还可以恢复活力。\n\n在浸泡过一种特殊的激素溶液后，§Y[From.GetName]§!的遗体被小心翼翼地埋入§Y[burial_planet.GetName]§!的土壤中。埋葬地由孵化者精心照料，他们耐心地等待幼体破土发芽，开始新的循环。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:570`

### English

The death of a §Y[From.Species.GetName]§! is not as final as it appears. Even if the main stem has begun to deteriorate, the roots can be reinvigorated.\n\nAfter soaking in a special hormone solution, §Y[From.GetName]§!'s remains are carefully buried in the soil of §Y[burial_planet.GetName]§!. The burial site is tended by brood members who patiently await for younglings to sprout on the surface, starting the cycle anew.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:686`

## paragon.563.a

## paragon.570.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.570.name&lang=en)

### 中文

死亡驱役

### English

Servitude in Death

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:689`

## paragon.570.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.570.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[From.GetTitle][From.GetName]§!在可靠地服务了[From.GetYearsOfService]后发生了故障，彻底报废。我们的科学家已经备份了文件以保留重要数据。\n\n基于§Y[From.GetName]§!的智能全息图谱已经被安装在我们的舰船系统中，作为虚拟顾问为我们服务。他们将继续支援我们的任务。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:573`

### English

§Y[From.GetTitle] [From.GetName]§! has broken down after [From.GetYearsOfService] of reliable service. Our scientists have backed up their files to preserve important data.\n\nAn intelligent hologram based on §Y[From.GetName]§! has been installed in our ship systems to serve as a virtual advisor. They will continue to aid in our mission.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:690`

## paragon.570.a

### English

## paragon.571.name

[root.GetGestaltEntityName]永存

### English

The [root.GetGestaltEntityName] Endures

## paragon.571.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.571.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[From.GetTitle][From.GetName]§!的意识在[From.GetAge]岁时从集体意识中移除。我们是§Y[Root.GetName]§!，我们将永存。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:576`

### English

The mind of §Y[From.GetTitle] [From.GetName]§! was removed from the collective at the age of [From.GetAge]. We are the §Y[Root.GetName]§!. We will endure.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:694`

## paragon.571.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.571.a&lang=en)

我们将永存。

### English
