## paragon.580.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.580.name&lang=en)

### 中文

大成智慧之殇

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:578`

### English

Death of a Great Mind

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:697`

## paragon.580.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.580.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们从§Y[system_killed_in.GetName]§!恒星系收到了噩耗。我们德高望重的科学家§Y[leader_killed.GetName]§!的舰队在一场残酷的战斗中被§Y[country_killed_by.GetName]§!彻底消灭。\n\n在最后的无私行动中，机组人员发出一条传输信息，其中包含了§Y[leader_killed.GetName]§!最新的研究成果。为纪念§Y[leader_killed.GetName]§!而举行的特别假期计划已经开始执行。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:579`

### English

We have received grave news from the §Y[system_killed_in.GetName]§! system. Our renowned scientist §Y[leader_killed.GetName]§!'s fleet was completely destroyed by §Y[country_killed_by.GetName]§! in a brutal battle.\n\nIn a final act of selflessness, the crew sent out a transmission containing all of §Y[leader_killed.GetName]§!'s latest research. Plans to honor §Y[leader_killed.GetName]§!'s memory with a special holiday have been set in motion.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:698`

## paragon.580.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.580.a&lang=en)

### 中文

牺牲没有白费。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:580`

### English

Their sacrifices were not in vain.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:699`

## paragon.581.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.581.name&lang=en)

### 中文

不可原谅的行径

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:581`

### English

An Unforgivable Act

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:701`

## paragon.581.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.581.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在§Y[country_killed_by.GetName]§!的恶意攻击下，我们伟大的科学家§Y[leader_killed.GetName]§!在§Y[system_killed_in.GetName]§!的战斗中陨落。\n\n这种野蛮的行径使我们全国上下震怒。我们的人民大声呼吁要讨回公道。我们要在能力的范围内千方百计确保§Y[country_killed_by.GetName]§!为这一天犯下的卑鄙行径而后悔。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:582`

### English

After a vicious attack by §Y[country_killed_by.GetName]§!, our great scientist §Y[leader_killed.GetName]§! has perished at the battle of §Y[system_killed_in.GetName]§!.\n\nThis act of brutality has shocked us profoundly. Our people are screaming for justice. We should do everything in our power to ensure §Y[country_killed_by.GetName]§! regrets this despicable act of violence.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:702`

## paragon.581.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.581.a&lang=en)

### 中文

绝对不能原谅。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:583`

### English

This shall not be forgotten.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:703`

## paragon.582.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.582.name&lang=en)

### 中文

著名科学官去世

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:584`

### English

Renowned Scientist Lost

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:705`

## paragon.582.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.582.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一位优秀的科学家最近从雷达屏幕上消失。在§Y[leader_killed.GetName]§!误入了§Y[system_killed_in.GetName]§!星系中的战斗后，我们很快与[leader_killed.GetHeShe]失去了联系。\n\n这对我们帝国是无可估量的损失。必须立刻采取措施预防此类损失的发生。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:585`

### English

A skilled scientist has recently fallen off the radar. We lost contact with §Y[leader_killed.GetName]§! after [leader_killed.GetHeShe] entered combat in the system of §Y[system_killed_in.GetName]§!.\n\nThis has been recorded as a significant loss to our empire. Measures to counter this loss must be taken immediately.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:706`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.582.a&lang=en)

### English

They must be replaced immediately.

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:595`

## paragon_death_good_tidings_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon_death_good_tidings_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这位长期在岗的领袖的去世，出乎意料地让许多互相敌对的派系关系紧密了起来。他们愿意互相协作，来解决这位逝去之人留下的烂摊子。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:597`

### English

The death of our long-serving leader has had the unexpected effect of bringing opposing parties closer. They are willing to work together to fix the mistakes of the deceased.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:722`

### English

The anatomical donation of our late leader has proved itself beneficial, continuing to bolster our academic endeavors.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:725`

### English

### 中文

一位伟大领袖的灵柩在军队的护驾下前往最终安息之地，我们的力量在星海中彰显。

A great leader has been escorted to their final resting place by a military parade, projecting our might to the galaxy.

Foundation of Peace

## paragon_death_foundation_peace_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon_death_foundation_peace_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

为了纪念这位已故的伟大领袖，人们建立了一个基金会来弥合我们社群之间的分歧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:605`

### English

To honor the late great leader, a foundation has been established to bridge the differences between our communities.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:734`

## paragon_death_reignited_traditions

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon_death_reignited_traditions_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

为追悼逝去的领袖举行的传统葬礼仪式，让人们想起了他们曾经团结一致的过往。这一事件提醒我们回顾历史，将我们同古老的传统方式再次连接在一起。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:607`

### English

The traditional funeral rites which marked the passing of our late leader have reminded the people of their united past. The event has reminded us of our history, and reconnected us to our old ways.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:737`

## paragon_death_to_the_stars

## paragon.1000.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.1000.name&lang=en)

### 中文

内阁失能

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:627`

### English

Dysfunctional Council

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:766`

## paragon.1000.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.1000.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一开始只是些许小小的争吵，现在已经演变成在每一项议题前都争论不休的僵局，这严重阻碍了我们内阁的运转效率。\n\n§Y[root.GetName]§!是内阁中最热衷于争吵和扰乱秩序的问题成员。许多人要求免去[root.GetHisHer]的席位，以恢复秩序和效率。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:628`

### English

What began as minor bickering has now devolved into quarrelsome gridlock on practically every issue brought before the Council, damaging its ability to function.\n\n§Y[root.GetName]§! has proven to be the most contentious and disruptive member of the Council. Many others are calling for [root.GetHisHer] removal so that order and efficiency can be restored.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:767`

## paragon.1000.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.1000.a&lang=en)

### 中文

开除那个惹麻烦的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:629`

### English

Dismiss the troublemaker.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:768`

## paragon.1000.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.1000.b&lang=en)

### 中文

他们必须学会协同工作。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:630`

### English

They must learn to work together.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:769`

## paragon.1005.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.1005.name&lang=en)

### 中文

宇宙现象激励

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:631`

### English

Inspired by Cosmic Phenomenon

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:771`

## paragon.1005.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.1005.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一种罕见的宇宙现象的壮观促使§Y[root.GetName]§!达到了新的高度。兴奋和新想法席卷而来，唯一的问题是应该专注于哪个科学分支。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:632`

### English

The majesty of a rarely seen cosmic phenomenon has spurred §Y[root.GetName]§! to new heights. Brimming with excitement and new ideas, the only question is what branch of science they should focus on.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:772`

## paragon.1005.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.1005.a&lang=en)

### 中文

物理来，物理去。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:633`

### English

Apply physics observations to physics.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:773`

## paragon.1005.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.1005.b&lang=en)

### 中文

重点放在社会学研究。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:634`

### English

Focus on societal research.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:774`

## paragon.1005.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.1005.c&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的工程师们应该更懂得怎么利用它。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:635`

### English

Our engineers know exactly what to do with this.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:775`

## paragon.1010.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.1010.name&lang=en)

### 中文

缴获违禁品

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:636`

### English

Confiscated Contraband

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:777`

## paragon.1010.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.1010.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在§Y[root.GetName]§!富有冒险精神的亲自部署、亲自指挥下，我们查获了大批违禁品。这次行动酝酿了很长时间，其成功地削弱了在我国境内扎根的有组织的犯罪集团。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:637`

### English

Under the audacious regime of §Y[root.GetName]§!, we have accumulated a huge cache of seized contraband. The operation was a long time in the making, and its successful conclusion has crippled the organized crime syndicates that had taken root within our borders.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:778`

## paragon.1010.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.1010.a&lang=en)

### 中文

正义虽迟但到。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:638`

### English

Justice never sleeps.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:779`

## paragon.1015.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.1015.name&lang=en)

### 中文

团结的内阁

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:639`

### English

Unified Council

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:781`

## paragon.1015.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.1015.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[event_target:leader_event_target.GetName]§!展现出了非凡的领导能力，将内阁团结了起来，确保了对我们社会的高效管理。充分运用统计学、传统和外交手段，[event_target:leader_event_target.GetName]成功地安抚了内阁的其他成员，以及我们社会中的各个派系。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:640`

### English

§Y[event_target:leader_event_target.GetName]§! has demonstrated  incredible leadership by uniting the council and ensuring the efficient administration of our society. Making use of statistics, tradition, and diplomacy, [event_target:leader_event_target.GetName] has managed to appease not only the other council members, but also our society's numerous factions.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:782`

## paragon.1015.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.1015.a&lang=en)

### 中文

携手共进，我们就会更加强大。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:641`

### English

We are stronger when we work together.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:783`

## paragon.1020.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.1020.name&lang=en)

### 中文

个人发展

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:642`

### English

Personal Development

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:785`

## paragon.1020.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.1020.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[event_target:leader_event_target.GetName]§!以自我发现和发展为目的开始了一段旅程。[event_target:leader_event_target.GetHisHer]的专心学习、刻苦钻研和善于自省，让[event_target:leader_event_target.GetHisHerCap]的心智能力达到了一个全新的高度。现在，[event_target:leader_event_target.GetName]决心挑战可能性的界限，并激励其他人去采摘星辰。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:643`

### English

§Y[event_target:leader_event_target.GetName]§! has embarked on a journey of self-discovery and personal development. [event_target:leader_event_target.GetHisHerCap] mental capabilities have reached new heights as a result of [event_target:leader_event_target.GetHisHer] focused study, hard work and introspection. Now, [event_target:leader_event_target.GetName] is determined to push the boundaries of what is possible and inspire others to reach for the stars.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:786`

## paragon.1020.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.1020.a&lang=en)

### 中文

鼓舞人心的工作态度。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:644`

### English

An inspiring work ethic.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:787`

## paragon.1025.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.1025.name&lang=en)

### 中文

科学进步

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:645`

### English

Scientific Progress

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:789`

## paragon.1025.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.1025.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[event_target:leader_event_target.GetName]§!令人震惊的科学发现直接导致研究的效率大幅提高。[event_target:leader_event_target.GetHisHerCap]的发现在科学圈中成了热门课题，他们现在将[event_target:leader_event_target.GetName]视为思想领袖。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:646`

### English

As a direct result of §Y[event_target:leader_event_target.GetName]§!'s astounding scientific discoveries, the rate at which we conduct research has accelerated. [event_target:leader_event_target.GetHisHerCap] findings are a hot topic in the scientific community, which now recognizes [event_target:leader_event_target.GetName] as a thought leader.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:790`

## paragon.1025.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.1025.a&lang=en)

### 中文

这些发展会让所有人收益。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:647`

### English

These developments will benefit all.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:791`

## paragon.1030.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.1030.name&lang=en)

### 中文

信息泄露

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:648`

### English

Leaked Information

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:793`

## paragon.1030.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.1030.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们发生了一次严重的信息泄露事故，许多敏感信息和关键的安保细节被公之于众。这些文件包含军事设施的编成表、神经交互界面实验、以及对几位重要的反对派人物的监视。\n\n§Y[root.GetName]§!在这次灾难事件中难辞其咎。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:649`

### English

A significant data leak has occurred, exposing sensitive information and crucial security details to the public. The files include schematics of military installations, details of neural interface experiments, and proof of surveillance of several prominent figures in the opposition.\n\n§Y[root.GetName]§! has been identified as the one responsible for this disaster.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:794`

## paragon.1030.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.1030.a&lang=en)

### 中文

出了乱子了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:650`

### English

Mistakes happen.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:795`

## paragon.1030.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.1030.b&lang=en)

查必严，责必究。

### English

## paragon.1035.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.1035.name&lang=en)

### 中文

时尚潮人

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:652`

### English

Trendsetter

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:798`

## paragon.1035.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.1035.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[event_target:leader_event_target.GetName]§!成为了一位对普罗大众而言的潮流开创者。从[event_target:leader_event_target.GetHisHer]的穿着风格到[event_target:leader_event_target.GetHisHer]的说话方式，一切都吸引了大批追随者。[event_target:leader_event_target.GetHisHerCap]的影响力扩展到了各个社会经济阶层，但[event_target:leader_event_target.GetSheHe]果然还是在年轻人中最受欢迎。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:653`

### English

§Y[event_target:leader_event_target.GetName]§! has become a trendsetter among the general population. Everything from [event_target:leader_event_target.GetHisHer] style to [event_target:leader_event_target.GetHisHer] method of speech has attracted a sizable following. [event_target:leader_event_target.GetHisHerCap] reach extends across all socioeconomic strata, but [event_target:leader_event_target.GetSheHe] has proven particularly popular among the youth.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:799`

## paragon.1035.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.1035.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们当然是支持了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:654`

### English

We have no reason not to support this.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:800`

## paragon.1040.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.1040.name&lang=en)

### 中文

永恒回声

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:655`

### English

Echo in Eternity

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:802`

## paragon.1040.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.1040.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[event_target:leader_event_target.GetName]§!发表的热情洋溢的演讲在掀起了一股爱国狂潮。[event_target:leader_event_target.GetHisHerCap]的赤子之心唤醒了我们全境内的责任感与民族热忱。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:656`

### English

The public have been swept up into a patriotic frenzy following a passionate speech delivered by §Y[event_target:leader_event_target.GetName]§!. [event_target:leader_event_target.GetHisHerCap] unbridled passion has heightened the sense of responsibility and nationalistic zeal that is now being felt throughout our territories.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:803`

## paragon.1040.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.1040.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们无可阻挡。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:657`

### English

Nothing can stop us now.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:804`

## paragon.1045.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.1045.name&lang=en)

### 中文

异质性倾向

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:658`

### English

Idiosyncratic Tendencies

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:806`

## paragon.1045.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.1045.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

统计分析表明§Y[event_target:leader_event_target.GetName]§!已经发展出认知和行为上的很大程度的异质性，使其趋近于一个独立个体。\n\n我们无法准确预测自由意志的危害和潜在益处。现在必须作出决定：[event_target:leader_event_target.GetName]应该保持现在的状态，还是应该强迫其回归正轨？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:659`

### English

Statistical analysis has confirmed that §Y[event_target:leader_event_target.GetName]§! has developed cognitive and behavioral idiosyncrasies of a magnitude that verge on the individual.\n\nThe risks and potential benefits of independent thought are beyond our ability to accurately predict. A decision has to be made: will [event_target:leader_event_target.GetName] be kept in this state, or should we force a correctional routine?

### English

Empire-Wide Scandal

## paragon.1050.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aparagon.1050.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[root.GetName]§!深陷一场轰动全国的巨大丑闻。关于不当行为，挪用公款和掩盖真相的报道层出不穷，许多人甚至因此质疑起了当权者的能力。\n\n毫无疑问，这些事件对公众情绪的影响还会持续好多年。许多人相信，恢复公信力的唯一手段就是让[root.GetName]下台并接受公审。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:663`

### English

§Y[root.GetName]§! is at the center of a major scandal reverberating throughout the empire. There are numerous reports of aberrant behavior, embezzlement, and cover-ups. This has led many to question the competence of those in authority.\n\nThe effects on the public mood will undoubtedly be felt for years to come. Many believe that the only way to restore faith in the system is for [root.GetName] to step down and face prosecution.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:812`

### English

## council_agenda_agenda_leadership_conditioning_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acouncil_agenda_agenda_leadership_conditioning_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们所有的领袖无论愿意与否都会花费可观的时间和精力进行内省和行为再培训。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1224`

### English

All our leaders spend, willingly or not, considerable time and effort on introspection and behavioral retraining.

## council_agenda_agenda_leadership_conditioning_desc_hive

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acouncil_agenda_agenda_leadership_conditioning_desc_hive&lang=en)

### 中文

我们领袖所蕴含的独特而仁慈的方面被聚集和统一到我们更高的意识中来。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1225`

### English

All unique, and benevolent, aspects of our leaders are gathered and unified into our higher consciousness.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:1405`

## council_agenda_agenda_leadership_conditioning_desc_machine

### English

All optimal non-standard procedures from leader units are analyzed, filtered, and finally combined with our core.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:1406`

## council_agenda_agenda_gestalt_purge_traits_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acouncil_agenda_agenda_gestalt_purge_traits_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

令人分散注意力的数据，以及错误的结论阻碍了我们的推理能力。我们必须清除所有多余的信息。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1305`

### English

Distracting data points, and false conclusions hinder our reasoning skills. We must purge all redundant information.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:1497`

## council_agenda_agenda_gestalt_purge_traits_effect_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acouncil_agenda_agenda_gestalt_purge_traits_effect_desc&lang=en)

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1306`

### English

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:1498`

### 中文

初步节点剔除

### English

Rudimentary Node Culling

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:1560`

## tech_node_culling_1_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_node_culling_1_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

随着星海舞台的风云变幻，我们的运筹本能也应如此。任何不再具有优势的进化位阶将被剔除。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1353`

### English

As the galactic stage evolves, so too should our operational instincts. Any evolutionary niche that no longer gives an advantage will be culled.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:1561`

## tech_node_culling_2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_node_culling_2&lang=en)

### 中文

基本节点剔除

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1354`

### English

Basic Node Culling

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:1562`

## tech_node_culling_2_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_node_culling_2_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

移除之后，会有一个替换性节点将被鼓励在合适的环境中成长起来。诀窍就是用前一个节点来滋养它。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1355`

### English

Once removed, a replacement node is encouraged to grow within the proper environment. The secret is to feed it the previous node.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:1563`

## tech_node_culling_3

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_node_culling_3&lang=en)

### 中文

先进节点剔除

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1356`

### English

Advanced Node Culling

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:1564`

## tech_node_culling_3_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_node_culling_3_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

踢出整个节点非常没有效率。相反，只需要修剪一些不需要的枝叶就行。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1357`

### English

Culling an entire node is very inefficient. Instead, just trim the divergent and unwanted lobes.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:1565`

## AGENDA_UNLOCK_NODE_CULLING_TITLE

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AAGENDA_UNLOCK_NODE_CULLING_TITLE&lang=en)

### 中文

§H解锁议程§!：节点剔除

### English

§HUnlocks Agendas§!: Node Culling

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:1566`

## AGENDA_UNLOCK_NODE_CULLING_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AAGENDA_UNLOCK_NODE_CULLING_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y剔除议程§!可以将不再适合我们目的的节点替换掉。这将把一个我们目前的节点替换成一个等级较低的节点。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1359`

### English

§YCulling Agendas§! allow us to replace Nodes that are no longer suited for our purpose. They will replace one of our current Nodes with a lower level Node.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:1567`

### 中文

初步节点格式化

## tech_node_reformatting_1_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_node_reformatting_1_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在快速发展的星系舞台，自决协议和制导算法可能会过时。它们将被像其他垃圾数据一样被覆盖掉。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1361`

### English

Self-determination protocols and guidance algorithms can become obsolete in a rapidly developing galactic stage. Like any junk data, they shall be overwritten.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:1570`

## tech_node_reformatting_2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_node_reformatting_2&lang=en)

### 中文

基本节点格式化

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1362`

### English

Basic Node Reformatting

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:1571`

## tech_node_reformatting_2_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_node_reformatting_2_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

每个自迭代算法都有它的价值。我们不会让重要的学识白白浪费在恢复我们的领袖身上。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1363`

### English

Every self-iterating algorithm has its value. We will not allow important learning go to waste when restoring our leaders.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:1572`

## tech_node_reformatting_3

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_node_reformatting_3&lang=en)

### 中文

### English

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:1573`

## tech_node_reformatting_3_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_node_reformatting_3_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

使用一个失败的软件来开发它的继任者可以快速重建。从错误的记忆路径中学习可以产生更高级的内阁指令。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1365`

### English

Using a failed piece of software to develop its successor enables quick reconstruction. Learning from faulty memory pathways generates even more advanced council directives.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_1_l_english.yml:1574`
